=== FILE: C:\Users\Виталя\Desktop\лудомания — копия\app\Http\Controllers\Admin\CaseController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\GameCase;
use App\Models\Skin;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class CaseController extends Controller
{
    public function index(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('admin/Cases/Index', [
            'cases' => GameCase::withCount('skins')->orderBy('name')->get()->map(fn (GameCase $gameCase) => [
                ...$gameCase->only(['id', 'name', 'price', 'category', 'featured', 'image']),
                'skinsCount' => $gameCase->skins_count,
            ]),
        ]);
    }

    public function create(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('admin/Cases/Form', [
            'caseItem' => null,
            'allSkins' => Skin::orderBy('name')->get()->map->toFrontend(),
            'categories' => $this->categories(),
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $this->validateCase($request);
        $gameCase = GameCase::create($validated);

        $this->syncSkins($gameCase, $request->input('skins', []));

        return redirect()->route('admin.cases.index')->with('success', 'РљРµР№СЃ СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅ.');
    }

    public function edit(GameCase $gameCase): Response
    {
        $gameCase->load('skins');

        return Inertia::render('admin/Cases/Form', [
            'caseItem' => [
                ...$gameCase->only(['id', 'name', 'price', 'image', 'category', 'featured']),
                'skins' => $gameCase->skins->map(fn (Skin $skin) => [
                    ...$skin->toFrontend(),
                    'dropWeight' => $skin->pivot->drop_weight,
                ]),
            ],
            'allSkins' => Skin::orderBy('name')->get()->map->toFrontend(),
            'categories' => $this->categories(),
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, GameCase $gameCase): RedirectResponse
    {
        $gameCase->update($this->validateCase($request));
        $this->syncSkins($gameCase, $request->input('skins', []));

        return redirect()->route('admin.cases.index')->with('success', 'РљРµР№СЃ РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»С‘РЅ.');
    }

    public function destroy(GameCase $gameCase): RedirectResponse
    {
        $gameCase->delete();

        return back()->with('success', 'РљРµР№СЃ СѓРґР°Р»С‘РЅ.');
    }

    private function validateCase(Request $request): array
    {
        return $request->validate([
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'price' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:1'],
            'image' => ['required', 'url', 'max:2048'],
            'category' => ['required', 'in:popular,new,premium,budget'],
            'featured' => ['boolean'],
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * @param  array<int, array{skin_id: int, drop_weight: int}>  $skins
     */
    private function syncSkins(GameCase $gameCase, array $skins): void
    {
        $sync = [];
        foreach ($skins as $item) {
            if (! empty($item['skin_id'])) {
                $sync[$item['skin_id']] = ['drop_weight' => $item['drop_weight'] ?? 100];
            }
        }
        $gameCase->skins()->sync($sync);
    }

    private function categories(): array
    {
        return [
            ['value' => 'popular', 'label' => 'РџРѕРїСѓР»СЏСЂРЅС‹Рµ'],
            ['value' => 'new', 'label' => 'РќРѕРІС‹Рµ'],
            ['value' => 'premium', 'label' => 'РџСЂРµРјРёСѓРј'],
            ['value' => 'budget', 'label' => 'Р‘СЋРґР¶РµС‚РЅС‹Рµ'],
        ];
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Skin;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class SkinController extends Controller
{
    public function index(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('admin/Skins/Index', [
            'skins' => Skin::orderBy('name')->get()->map->toFrontend(),
            'rarities' => $this->rarities(),
        ]);
    }

    public function create(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('admin/Skins/Form', [
            'skin' => null,
            'rarities' => $this->rarities(),
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $this->validateSkin($request);
        Skin::create($validated);

        return redirect()->route('admin.skins.index')->with('success', 'РЎРєРёРЅ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅ РІ РїСѓР».');
    }

    public function edit(Skin $skin): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('admin/Skins/Form', [
            'skin' => $skin->toFrontend(),
            'rarities' => $this->rarities(),
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, Skin $skin): RedirectResponse
    {
        $skin->update($this->validateSkin($request));

        return redirect()->route('admin.skins.index')->with('success', 'РЎРєРёРЅ РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»С‘РЅ.');
    }

    public function destroy(Skin $skin): RedirectResponse
    {
        $skin->delete();

        return back()->with('success', 'РЎРєРёРЅ СѓРґР°Р»С‘РЅ РёР· РїСѓР»Р°.');
    }

    private function validateSkin(Request $request): array
    {
        return $request->validate([
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'weapon' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'rarity' => ['required', 'in:consumer,industrial,mil-spec,restricted,classified,covert,gold'],
            'price' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:1'],
            'image' => ['required', 'url', 'max:2048'],
        ]);
    }

    private function rarities(): array
    {
        return [
            ['value' => 'consumer', 'label' => 'Consumer Grade'],
            ['value' => 'industrial', 'label' => 'Industrial Grade'],
            ['value' => 'mil-spec', 'label' => 'Mil-Spec'],
            ['value' => 'restricted', 'label' => 'Restricted'],
            ['value' => 'classified', 'label' => 'Classified'],
            ['value' => 'covert', 'label' => 'Covert'],
            ['value' => 'gold', 'label' => 'в… Rare Special'],
        ];
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\Виталя\Desktop\лудомания — копия\app\Http\Controllers\Admin\UserController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Enums\UserRole;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class UserController extends Controller
{
    public function index(): Response
    {
        $users = User::query()
            ->orderByRaw("CASE role WHEN 'admin' THEN 1 WHEN 'manager' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END")
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->get()
            ->map->toAdminFrontend();

        return Inertia::render('admin/Users/Index', [
            'users' => $users,
            'roles' => collect(UserRole::cases())->map(fn (UserRole $role) => [
                'value' => $role->value,
                'label' => $role->label(),
            ])->values(),
        ]);
    }

    public function updateRole(Request $request, User $user): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'role' => ['required', 'in:user,manager,admin'],
        ]);

        if ($user->id === $request->user()->id && $validated['role'] !== UserRole::Admin->value) {
            return back()->with('error', 'РќРµР»СЊР·СЏ РїРѕРЅРёР·РёС‚СЊ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅСѓСЋ СЂРѕР»СЊ.');
        }

        $user->update(['role' => $validated['role']]);

        return back()->with('success', "Р РѕР»СЊ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЏ {$user->name} РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅР°.");
    }

    public function destroy(Request $request, User $user): RedirectResponse
    {
        if ($user->id === $request->user()->id) {
            return back()->with('error', 'РќРµР»СЊР·СЏ СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СЃРІРѕР№ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚.');
        }

        $user->delete();

        return back()->with('success', 'РџРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ СѓРґР°Р»С‘РЅ.');
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\Виталя\Desktop\лудомания — копия\app\Http\Controllers\Api\AdminController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\GameCase;
use App\Models\InventoryItem;
use App\Models\LiveDrop;
use App\Models\Skin;
use App\Models\SkinWithdrawal;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Models\UserSkinHistory;
use App\Support\GameEconomy;
use App\Support\GameImages;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class AdminController extends Controller
{
    public function stats()
    {
        return [
            'users' => User::count(),
            'superAdmins' => User::where('role', 'super_admin')->count(),
            'admins' => User::where('role', 'admin')->count(),
            'managers' => User::where('role', 'manager')->count(),
            'skins' => Skin::count(),
            'cases' => GameCase::count(),
            'opensToday' => LiveDrop::whereDate('created_at', today())->count(),
        ];
    }

    public function skins()
    {
        return Skin::orderBy('name')->get()->map->toFrontend();
    }

    public function storeSkin(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $this->validateSkin($request);
        $validated['image'] = $this->resolveSkinImage($request, null);

        $skin = Skin::create($validated);

        return response()->json($skin->toFrontend());
    }

    public function updateSkin(Request $request, Skin $skin)
    {
        $validated = $this->validateSkin($request);
        $validated['image'] = $this->resolveSkinImage($request, $skin->image);
        $skin->update($validated);

        return response()->json($skin->fresh()->toFrontend());
    }

    public function uploadSkinImage(Request $request, Skin $skin)
    {
        $request->validate(['image' => 'required|image|max:2048']);
        $path = GameImages::storeUpload($request->file('image'), 'skins', $skin->image);
        $skin->update(['image' => $path ?? $skin->image]);

        return response()->json($skin->fresh()->toFrontend());
    }

    public function deleteSkin(Skin $skin)
    {
        $skin->delete();

        return response()->json(['message' => 'РЎРєРёРЅ СѓРґР°Р»С‘РЅ']);
    }

    public function cases()
    {
        return GameCase::withCount('skins')
            ->where('category', '!=', 'secret')
            ->orderByDesc('price')
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->get()
            ->map(fn (GameCase $gameCase) => $this->caseToAdmin($gameCase));
    }

    public function showCase(GameCase $gameCase)
    {
        if ($gameCase->category === 'secret') {
            abort(404);
        }

        $gameCase->load('skins');

        return $this->caseToAdmin($gameCase, true);
    }

    public function storeCase(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $this->validateCase($request);
        $validated['image'] = $this->resolveCaseImage($request, null);
        $validated['featured'] = (bool) ($validated['featured'] ?? false);
        $gameCase = GameCase::create($validated);
        try {
            $this->syncSkins($gameCase, $request->input('skins', []));
        } catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
            $gameCase->delete();

            return response()->json(['message' => $e->getMessage()], 422);
        }

        return response()->json($this->caseToAdmin($gameCase->fresh()->loadCount('skins')->load('skins'), true));
    }

    public function updateCase(Request $request, GameCase $gameCase)
    {
        if ($gameCase->category === 'secret') {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'РЎРµРєСЂРµС‚РЅС‹Р№ РєРµР№СЃ РЅРµР»СЊР·СЏ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ.'], 422);
        }

        $validated = $this->validateCase($request);
        $validated['image'] = $this->resolveCaseImage($request, $gameCase->image);
        $validated['featured'] = (bool) ($validated['featured'] ?? false);
        $gameCase->update($validated);

        try {
            $this->syncSkins($gameCase, $request->input('skins', []));
        } catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
            return response()->json(['message' => $e->getMessage()], 422);
        }

        return response()->json($this->caseToAdmin($gameCase->fresh()->loadCount('skins')->load('skins'), true));
    }

    public function uploadCaseImage(Request $request, GameCase $gameCase)
    {
        if ($gameCase->category === 'secret') {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'РЎРµРєСЂРµС‚РЅС‹Р№ РєРµР№СЃ РЅРµР»СЊР·СЏ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ.'], 422);
        }

        $request->validate(['image' => 'required|image|max:2048']);
        $path = GameImages::storeUpload($request->file('image'), 'cases', $gameCase->image);
        $gameCase->update(['image' => $path ?? $gameCase->image]);

        return response()->json($this->caseToAdmin($gameCase->fresh()->loadCount('skins')->load('skins'), true));
    }

    public function deleteCase(GameCase $gameCase)
    {
        $gameCase->delete();

        return response()->json(['message' => 'РљРµР№СЃ СѓРґР°Р»С‘РЅ']);
    }

    public function withdrawals()
    {
        return SkinWithdrawal::query()
            ->with('user')
            ->latest()
            ->limit(200)
            ->get()
            ->map->toFrontend();
    }

    public function updateWithdrawalStatus(Request $request, SkinWithdrawal $withdrawal)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'status' => 'required|in:pending,processing,completed,cancelled',
        ]);

        $newStatus = $validated['status'];

        if ($withdrawal->status === SkinWithdrawal::STATUS_COMPLETED
            || $withdrawal->status === SkinWithdrawal::STATUS_CANCELLED) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'Р—Р°СЏРІРєР° СѓР¶Рµ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚Р°.'], 422);
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($withdrawal, $newStatus) {
            $withdrawal = SkinWithdrawal::query()->lockForUpdate()->findOrFail($withdrawal->id);

            if ($newStatus === SkinWithdrawal::STATUS_CANCELLED) {
                InventoryItem::create([
                    'user_id' => $withdrawal->user_id,
                    'skin_id' => $withdrawal->skin_id,
                    'source' => 'withdraw_return',
                ]);

                UserSkinHistory::query()
                    ->where('user_id', $withdrawal->user_id)
                    ->where('skin_id', $withdrawal->skin_id)
                    ->where('status', 'withdraw_pending')
                    ->latest()
                    ->limit(1)
                    ->update(['status' => 'inventory']);

                $withdrawal->update(['status' => SkinWithdrawal::STATUS_CANCELLED]);

                return response()->json($withdrawal->fresh('user')->toFrontend());
            }

            if ($newStatus === SkinWithdrawal::STATUS_COMPLETED
                && $withdrawal->status !== SkinWithdrawal::STATUS_COMPLETED) {
                UserSkinHistory::query()
                    ->where('user_id', $withdrawal->user_id)
                    ->where('skin_id', $withdrawal->skin_id)
                    ->where('status', 'withdraw_pending')
                    ->latest()
                    ->limit(1)
                    ->update(['status' => 'withdrawn']);

                $withdrawal->user->incrementStat('withdraw_total', $withdrawal->skin_price);
            }

            $withdrawal->update(['status' => $newStatus]);

            return response()->json($withdrawal->fresh('user')->toFrontend());
        });
    }

    private function validateSkin(Request $request): array
    {
        return $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'weapon' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'rarity' => 'required|in:consumer,industrial,mil-spec,restricted,classified,covert,gold',
            'price' => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'image' => 'nullable|string|max:2048',
        ]);
    }

    private function validateCase(Request $request): array
    {
        return $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'price' => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'image' => 'nullable|string|max:2048',
            'category' => 'required|in:serial,branded,image,knife,valve,limited,bonus,premium,popular,new,budget',
            'featured' => 'sometimes|boolean',
            'skins' => 'sometimes|array',
            'skins.*.skin_id' => 'required_with:skins|integer|exists:skins,id',
            'skins.*.drop_weight' => 'nullable|integer|min:1',
        ]);
    }

    private function syncSkins(GameCase $gameCase, array $skins): void
    {
        $sync = [];
        foreach ($skins as $item) {
            if (empty($item['skin_id'])) {
                continue;
            }

            $skin = Skin::find($item['skin_id']);
            if (! $skin) {
                continue;
            }

            $minPrice = GameEconomy::minSkinPriceForCase((int) $gameCase->price, $gameCase->category);
            if ($minPrice > 0 && (int) $skin->price < $minPrice) {
                continue;
            }

            $sync[$skin->id] = [
                'drop_weight' => $item['drop_weight'] ?? GameEconomy::dropWeightForRarity($skin->rarity),
            ];
        }
        $gameCase->skins()->sync($sync);

        if ((int) $gameCase->price >= GameEconomy::CASE_MIN_SKIN_PRICE_THRESHOLD && ! empty($sync)) {
            $cheapCount = $gameCase->skins()
                ->get()
                ->filter(fn (Skin $skin) => GameEconomy::isCheapTierSkinPrice((int) $skin->price, (int) $gameCase->price, $gameCase->category))
                ->count();

            if ($cheapCount < GameEconomy::CASE_CHEAP_SKIN_COUNT) {
                throw new \RuntimeException(
                    'Р’ РєРµР№СЃРµ РґРѕР»Р¶РЅРѕ Р±С‹С‚СЊ РјРёРЅРёРјСѓРј '.GameEconomy::CASE_CHEAP_SKIN_COUNT
                    .' СЃРєРёРЅРѕРІ РІ РґРёР°РїР°Р·РѕРЅРµ 50%вЂ“80% РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹ РєРµР№СЃР° (СЃРµР№С‡Р°СЃ: '.$cheapCount.').'
                );
            }
        }
    }

    private function resolveSkinImage(Request $request, ?string $current): string
    {
        if ($request->hasFile('image')) {
            $request->validate(['image' => 'image|max:2048']);

            return GameImages::storeUpload($request->file('image'), 'skins', $current)
                ?? GameImages::SKIN_PLACEHOLDER;
        }

        if ($request->filled('image')) {
            return $request->string('image')->toString();
        }

        return $current ?? GameImages::SKIN_PLACEHOLDER;
    }

    private function resolveCaseImage(Request $request, ?string $current): string
    {
        if ($request->hasFile('image')) {
            $request->validate(['image' => 'image|max:2048']);

            return GameImages::storeUpload($request->file('image'), 'cases', $current)
                ?? GameImages::CASE_PLACEHOLDER;
        }

        if ($request->filled('image')) {
            return $request->string('image')->toString();
        }

        return $current ?? GameImages::CASE_PLACEHOLDER;
    }

    private function caseToAdmin(GameCase $gameCase, bool $withSkins = false): array
    {
        $data = [
            'id' => $gameCase->id,
            'name' => $gameCase->name,
            'price' => $gameCase->price,
            'category' => $gameCase->category,
            'featured' => (bool) $gameCase->featured,
            'image' => GameImages::case($gameCase->image),
            'skins_count' => $gameCase->skins_count ?? $gameCase->skins()->count(),
        ];

        if ($withSkins) {
            $gameCase->loadMissing('skins');
            $data['skins'] = $gameCase->skins->map(fn (Skin $skin) => [
                'skin_id' => $skin->id,
                'drop_weight' => (int) ($skin->pivot->drop_weight ?? GameEconomy::dropWeightForRarity($skin->rarity)),
                ...$skin->toFrontend(),
            ])->values()->all();
        }

        return $data;
    }
}

    public function cases()
    {

    public function secretCase(Request $request)
    {
        $gameCase = GameCase::with('skins')
            ->where('category', 'secret')
            ->firstOrFail();

        $cooldown = CaseOpenService::SECRET_CASE_COOLDOWN_SECONDS;
        $readyAt = null;
        $secondsLeft = 0;

        if ($request->user()) {
            $lastAt = $request->user()->stat()->last_secret_case_at;
            if ($lastAt) {
                $readyAt = $lastAt->copy()->addSeconds($cooldown)->timestamp;
                $secondsLeft = max(0, $readyAt - time());
            }
        }

        return response()->json([
            'case' => $gameCase->toFrontend(),
            'cooldownSeconds' => $cooldown,
            'readyAt' => $readyAt,
            'secondsLeft' => $secondsLeft,
            'legendaryPromo' => $this->promoCodeService->getLegendaryPromo(),
        ]);
    }

    public function openSecretCase(Request $request)
    {
        $gameCase = GameCase::where('category', 'secret')->firstOrFail();

        try {
            return response()->json($this->caseOpenService->openSecret($request->user(), $gameCase));
        } catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
            return response()->json(['message' => $e->getMessage()], 422);
        }
    }

    public function deposit(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'amount' => 'required|integer|min:1|max:1000000',
            'promo_code' => 'nullable|string|max:32',
        ]);

        $credit = $this->promoCodeService->calculateCredit(
            $validated['amount'],
            $validated['promo_code'] ?? null,
        );

        if ($validated['promo_code'] && ! $credit['promo']) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'РџСЂРѕРјРѕРєРѕРґ РЅРµРґРµР№СЃС‚РІРёС‚РµР»РµРЅ РёР»Рё РёСЃС‚С‘Рє.'], 422);
        }

        $user = $this->balanceService->depositWithPromo(
            $request->user(),
            $credit['total'],
            $credit['amount'],
            $credit['bonus'],
            $credit['promo']['code'] ?? null,
        );

        return response()->json([
            'balance' => $user->balance,
            'credited' => $credit['total'],
            'bonus' => $credit['bonus'],
            'bonusPercent' => $credit['bonusPercent'],
        ]);
    }

    public function withdrawSkins(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'inventory_item_ids' => 'required|array|min:1|max:20',
            'inventory_item_ids.*' => 'integer|exists:inventory_items,id',
        ]);

        $items = InventoryItem::with('skin')
            ->where('user_id', $request->user()->id)
            ->whereIn('id', $validated['inventory_item_ids'])
            ->get();

        if ($items->count() !== count($validated['inventory_item_ids'])) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'РќРµРєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅС‹Р№ РІС‹Р±РѕСЂ СЃРєРёРЅРѕРІ.'], 422);
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $items) {
            $user = $request->user();
            $totalValue = 0;
            $withdrawals = [];

            foreach ($items as $item) {
                $price = $item->skin->price;
                $totalValue += $price;

                SkinWithdrawal::create([
                    'user_id' => $user->id,
                    'skin_id' => $item->skin_id,
                    'skin_price' => $price,
                    'weapon' => $item->skin->weapon,
                    'skin_name' => $item->skin->name,
                    'status' => SkinWithdrawal::STATUS_PENDING,
                ]);

                $this->skinHistoryService->markByInventoryItem($item->id, 'withdraw_pending');
                $item->delete();
            }

            return response()->json([
                'message' => 'Р—Р°СЏРІРєР° РЅР° РІС‹РІРѕРґ СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅР°. РЎРєРёРЅС‹ Р±СѓРґСѓС‚ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅС‹ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРё Р°РґРјРёРЅРѕРј.',
                'skinsValue' => $totalValue,
                'count' => $items->count(),
                'withdrawals' => SkinWithdrawal::query()
                    ->where('user_id', $user->id)
                    ->latest()
                    ->limit($items->count())
                    ->get()
                    ->map->toFrontend(),
            ]);
        });
    }

    public function openCase(Request $request, int $id)

        } catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
            return response()->json(['message' => $e->getMessage()], 422);
        }
    }

    public function upgrade(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'inventory_item_id' => 'required|integer|exists:inventory_items,id',
            'target_skin_id' => 'required|integer|exists:skins,id',
            'boost_amount' => 'sometimes|integer|min:0|max:50000',
        ]);

        $boostAmount = $validated['boost_amount'] ?? 0;

        $item = InventoryItem::with('skin')
            ->where('user_id', $request->user()->id)
            ->findOrFail($validated['inventory_item_id']);

        $targetSkin = Skin::findOrFail($validated['target_skin_id']);

        if ($targetSkin->price <= $item->skin->price) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'Р¦РµР»РµРІРѕР№ СЃРєРёРЅ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ Р±С‹С‚СЊ РґРѕСЂРѕР¶Рµ РІР°С€РµРіРѕ.'], 422);
        }

        if (! GameEconomy::isUpgradeTargetAllowed($item->skin->price, $targetSkin->price)) {
            return response()->json([
                'message' => 'РќРµР»СЊР·СЏ Р°РїРіСЂРµР№РґРёС‚СЊ РЅР° СЌС‚РѕС‚ СЃРєРёРЅ: Р±Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Р№ С€Р°РЅСЃ РІС‹С€Рµ '.GameEconomy::UPGRADE_BASE_MAX.'%.',
            ], 422);
        }

        if ($boostAmount > $request->user()->balance) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'РќРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ Р±Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃР° РґР»СЏ Р±СѓСЃС‚Р°.'], 422);
        }

        $maxBoost = GameEconomy::maxUpgradeBoost(
            $item->skin->price,
            $targetSkin->price,
            $request->user()->balance,
        );

        if ($boostAmount > $maxBoost) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'Р‘СѓСЃС‚ РїСЂРµРІС‹С€Р°РµС‚ РґРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёРјС‹Р№ РґР»СЏ СЌС‚РѕР№ РїР°СЂС‹ СЃРєРёРЅРѕРІ.'], 422);
        }

        $chance = GameEconomy::upgradeChance($item->skin->price, $targetSkin->price, $boostAmount);
        $success = GameEconomy::upgradeRollSuccess($chance);

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $item, $targetSkin, $success, $chance, $boostAmount) {
            $user = $request->user();

            if ($boostAmount > 0) {
                $this->balanceService->deduct(
                    $user,
                    $boostAmount,
                    'upgrade_boost',
                    "Р‘СѓСЃС‚ Р°РїРіСЂРµР№РґР° (+{$boostAmount} в‚Ѕ)",
                );
                $user = $user->fresh();
            }

            $inventoryItemId = $item->id;
            $this->skinHistoryService->markByInventoryItem($inventoryItemId, 'upgrade');
            $item->delete();
            $user->incrementStat('upgrades_count');

            if ($success) {
                $user->incrementStat('upgrades_success');

                $newItem = InventoryItem::create([
                    'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
                    'skin_id' => $targetSkin->id,
                    'source' => 'upgrade',
                ]);

                $this->skinHistoryService->recordAcquisition(
                    $user,
                    $targetSkin,
                    'upgrade',
                    'inventory',
                    $newItem->id,
                );

                return response()->json([
                    'success' => true,
                    'skin' => $targetSkin->toFrontend(),
                    'chance' => $chance,
                ]);
            }

            return response()->json([
                'success' => false,
                'chance' => $chance,
            ]);
        });
    }

    public function contract(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'inventory_item_ids' => 'required|array|min:2|max:10',
            'inventory_item_ids.*' => 'integer|exists:inventory_items,id',
            'boost_amount' => 'sometimes|integer|min:0|max:50000',
        ]);

        $boostAmount = $validated['boost_amount'] ?? 0;

        if ($boostAmount > $request->user()->balance) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'РќРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ Р±Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃР° РґР»СЏ Р±СѓСЃС‚Р°.'], 422);
        }

        $items = InventoryItem::with('skin')
            ->where('user_id', $request->user()->id)
            ->whereIn('id', $validated['inventory_item_ids'])
            ->get();

        if ($items->count() !== count($validated['inventory_item_ids'])) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'РќРµРєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅС‹Р№ РІС‹Р±РѕСЂ СЃРєРёРЅРѕРІ.'], 422);
        }

        $totalValue = $items->sum(fn ($item) => $item->skin->price);
        $range = GameEconomy::contractRange($totalValue, $items->count(), $boostAmount);
        $minPrice = $range['min'];
        $maxPrice = $range['max'];

        $candidates = Skin::query()
            ->whereBetween('price', [$minPrice, $maxPrice])
            ->get();

        if ($candidates->isEmpty()) {
            return response()->json([
                'message' => "РќРµС‚ СЃРєРёРЅРѕРІ РІ РґРёР°РїР°Р·РѕРЅРµ {$minPrice}вЂ“{$maxPrice} в‚Ѕ РґР»СЏ РєРѕРЅС‚СЂР°РєС‚Р°.",
            ], 422);
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $items, $candidates, $minPrice, $maxPrice, $boostAmount) {
            $user = $request->user();

            if ($boostAmount > 0) {
                $this->balanceService->deduct(
                    $user,
                    $boostAmount,
                    'contract_boost',
                    "Р‘СѓСЃС‚ РєРѕРЅС‚СЂР°РєС‚Р° (+{$boostAmount} в‚Ѕ)",
                );
            }

            $itemIds = $items->pluck('id')->all();
            $this->skinHistoryService->markManyByInventoryItems($itemIds, 'contract');
            InventoryItem::whereIn('id', $itemIds)->delete();
            $user->incrementStat('contracts_count');

            $newSkin = GameEconomy::pickContractSkin($candidates);

            $newItem = InventoryItem::create([
                'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
                'skin_id' => $newSkin->id,
                'source' => 'contract',
            ]);

            $this->skinHistoryService->recordAcquisition(
                $user,
                $newSkin,
                'contract',
                'inventory',
                $newItem->id,
            );

            return response()->json([
                'skin' => $newSkin->toFrontend(),
                'range' => ['min' => $minPrice, 'max' => $maxPrice],
            ]);
        });
    }

    public function sellItem(Request $request, int $id)
    {
        $item = InventoryItem::with('skin')
            ->where('user_id', $request->user()->id)
            ->findOrFail($id);

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $item) {
            $price = $item->skin->price;
            $this->skinHistoryService->markByInventoryItem($item->id, 'sold');
            $item->delete();
            $request->user()->increment('balance', $price);

            BalanceTransaction::create([
                'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
                'amount' => $price,
                'type' => 'sell',
                'description' => "РџСЂРѕРґР°Р¶Р°: {$item->skin->weapon} | {$item->skin->name}",
            ]);

            return response()->json([
                'balance' => $request->user()->fresh()->balance,
                'amount' => $price,
            ]);
        });
    }

    public function sellAllItems(Request $request)
    {
        $items = InventoryItem::with('skin')
            ->where('user_id', $request->user()->id)
            ->get();

        if ($items->isEmpty()) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'РРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂСЊ РїСѓСЃС‚.'], 422);
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $items) {
            $user = $request->user();
            $total = 0;
            $count = $items->count();
            $itemIds = $items->pluck('id')->all();

            foreach ($items as $item) {
                $total += $item->skin->price;
            }

            $this->skinHistoryService->markManyByInventoryItems($itemIds, 'sold');
            InventoryItem::query()->whereIn('id', $itemIds)->delete();
            $user->increment('balance', $total);

            BalanceTransaction::create([
                'user_id' => $user->id,
                'amount' => $total,
                'type' => 'sell',
                'description' => "РџСЂРѕРґР°Р¶Р° РІСЃРµС… СЃРєРёРЅРѕРІ ({$count} С€С‚.)",
            ]);

            return response()->json([
                'balance' => $user->fresh()->balance,
                'amount' => $total,
                'count' => $count,
            ]);
        });
    }

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\GameCase;
use App\Models\LiveDrop;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class ManagerController extends Controller
{
    public function stats()
    {
        return [
            'totalUsers' => User::where('role', 'user')->count(),
            'totalCases' => GameCase::count(),
            'featuredCases' => GameCase::where('featured', true)->count(),
            'opensToday' => LiveDrop::whereDate('created_at', today())->count(),
            'totalBalance' => User::sum('balance'),
        ];
    }

    public function cases()
    {
        return GameCase::withCount('skins')
            ->where('category', '!=', 'secret')
            ->orderByDesc('price')
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->get();
    }

    public function toggleFeatured(GameCase $gameCase)
    {
        $gameCase->update(['featured' => ! $gameCase->featured]);

        return response()->json($gameCase->fresh());
    }

    public function liveDrops()
    {
        return LiveDrop::query()
            ->with(['user', 'skin', 'gameCase'])
            ->latest()
            ->limit(100)
            ->get()
            ->map->toFrontend();
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Manager;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\GameCase;
use App\Models\Skin;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class CaseController extends Controller
{
    public function index(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('manager/Cases/Index', [
            'cases' => GameCase::withCount('skins')->orderBy('name')->get()->map(fn (GameCase $gameCase) => [
                ...$gameCase->only(['id', 'name', 'price', 'category', 'featured', 'image']),
                'skinsCount' => $gameCase->skins_count,
            ]),
        ]);
    }

    public function edit(GameCase $gameCase): Response
    {
        $gameCase->load('skins');

        return Inertia::render('manager/Cases/Form', [
            'caseItem' => [
                ...$gameCase->only(['id', 'name', 'price', 'image', 'category', 'featured']),
                'skins' => $gameCase->skins->map(fn (Skin $skin) => [
                    ...$skin->toFrontend(),
                    'dropWeight' => $skin->pivot->drop_weight,
                ]),
            ],
            'allSkins' => Skin::orderBy('name')->get()->map->toFrontend(),
            'categories' => $this->categories(),
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, GameCase $gameCase): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'price' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:1'],
            'image' => ['required', 'url', 'max:2048'],
            'category' => ['required', 'in:popular,new,premium,budget'],
            'featured' => ['boolean'],
        ]);

        $gameCase->update($validated);
        $this->syncSkins($gameCase, $request->input('skins', []));

        return redirect()->route('manager.cases.index')->with('success', 'РљРµР№СЃ РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»С‘РЅ.');
    }

    public function toggleFeatured(GameCase $gameCase): RedirectResponse
    {
        $gameCase->update(['featured' => ! $gameCase->featured]);

        return back()->with('success', $gameCase->featured ? 'РљРµР№СЃ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅ РІ Hot' : 'РљРµР№СЃ СѓР±СЂР°РЅ РёР· Hot');
    }

    /**
     * @param  array<int, array{skin_id: int, drop_weight: int}>  $skins
     */
    private function syncSkins(GameCase $gameCase, array $skins): void
    {
        $sync = [];
        foreach ($skins as $item) {
            if (! empty($item['skin_id'])) {
                $sync[$item['skin_id']] = ['drop_weight' => $item['drop_weight'] ?? 100];
            }
        }
        $gameCase->skins()->sync($sync);
    }

    private function categories(): array
    {
        return [
            ['value' => 'popular', 'label' => 'РџРѕРїСѓР»СЏСЂРЅС‹Рµ'],
            ['value' => 'new', 'label' => 'РќРѕРІС‹Рµ'],
            ['value' => 'premium', 'label' => 'РџСЂРµРјРёСѓРј'],
            ['value' => 'budget', 'label' => 'Р‘СЋРґР¶РµС‚РЅС‹Рµ'],
        ];
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Settings;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Settings\ProfileDeleteRequest;
use App\Http\Requests\Settings\ProfileUpdateRequest;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Auth\MustVerifyEmail;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class ProfileController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Show the user's profile settings page.
     */
    public function edit(Request $request): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('settings/Profile', [
            'mustVerifyEmail' => $request->user() instanceof MustVerifyEmail,
            'status' => $request->session()->get('status'),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Update the user's profile information.
     */
    public function update(ProfileUpdateRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->user()->fill($request->validated());

        if ($request->user()->isDirty('email')) {
            $request->user()->email_verified_at = null;
        }

        $request->user()->save();

        return to_route('profile.edit');
    }

    /**
     * Delete the user's profile.
     */
    public function destroy(ProfileDeleteRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        Auth::logout();

        $user->delete();

        $request->session()->invalidate();
        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return redirect('/');
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\Виталя\Desktop\лудомания — копия\app\Http\Controllers\Settings\TwoFactorAuthenticationController.php ===
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Settings;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Settings\TwoFactorAuthenticationRequest;
use Illuminate\Routing\Controllers\HasMiddleware;
use Illuminate\Routing\Controllers\Middleware;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;
use Laravel\Fortify\Features;

class TwoFactorAuthenticationController extends Controller implements HasMiddleware
{
    /**
     * Get the middleware that should be assigned to the controller.
     */
    public static function middleware(): array
    {
        return Features::optionEnabled(Features::twoFactorAuthentication(), 'confirmPassword')
            ? [new Middleware('password.confirm', only: ['show'])]
            : [];
    }

    /**
     * Show the user's two-factor authentication settings page.
     */
    public function show(TwoFactorAuthenticationRequest $request): Response
    {
        $request->ensureStateIsValid();

        return Inertia::render('settings/TwoFactor', [
            'twoFactorEnabled' => $request->user()->hasEnabledTwoFactorAuthentication(),
            'requiresConfirmation' => Features::optionEnabled(Features::twoFactorAuthentication(), 'confirm'),
        ]);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\GameCase;
use App\Services\CaseOpenService;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class CaseOpenController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private CaseOpenService $caseOpenService) {}

    public function open(Request $request, int $id): JsonResponse
    {
        $gameCase = GameCase::with('skins')->findOrFail($id);

        try {
            $result = $this->caseOpenService->open($request->user(), $gameCase);
        } catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
            return response()->json(['message' => $e->getMessage()], 422);
        }

        return response()->json($result);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\GameCase;
use App\Models\InventoryItem;
use App\Models\LiveDrop;
use App\Models\Skin;
use App\Support\GameEconomy;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class GameController extends Controller
{
    public function home(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Home', [
            'cases' => GameCase::with('skins')->get()->map->toFrontend(),
            'liveDrops' => LiveDrop::recent(20)->map->toFrontend(),
        ]);
    }

    public function battles(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Battles', [
            'battles' => $this->mockBattles(),
            'liveDrops' => LiveDrop::recent(20)->map->toFrontend(),
        ]);
    }

    public function upgrade(Request $request): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Upgrade', [
            'liveDrops' => LiveDrop::recent(20)->map->toFrontend(),
            'inventory' => $this->userInventory($request),
        ]);
    }

    public function contracts(Request $request): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Contracts', [
            'liveDrops' => LiveDrop::recent(20)->map->toFrontend(),
            'inventory' => $this->userInventory($request),
        ]);
    }

    public function showCase(int $id): Response
    {
        $gameCase = GameCase::with('skins')->findOrFail($id);

        return Inertia::render('Case/Show', [
            'caseItem' => $gameCase->toFrontend(),
            'liveDrops' => LiveDrop::recent(20)->map->toFrontend(),
        ]);
    }

    public function performUpgrade(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'inventory_item_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:inventory_items,id'],
            'target_price' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:1'],
        ]);

        $item = InventoryItem::with('skin')
            ->where('user_id', $request->user()->id)
            ->findOrFail($validated['inventory_item_id']);

        $chance = GameEconomy::upgradeChance($item->skin->price, $validated['target_price'], 0);
        $success = GameEconomy::upgradeRollSuccess($chance);

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $item, $validated, $success) {
            $item->delete();

            if ($success) {
                $newSkin = Skin::query()
                    ->where('price', '<=', $validated['target_price'])
                    ->where('price', '>=', $item->skin->price)
                    ->inRandomOrder()
                    ->first() ?? $item->skin;

                InventoryItem::create([
                    'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
                    'skin_id' => $newSkin->id,
                    'source' => 'upgrade',
                ]);

                return response()->json([
                    'success' => true,
                    'skin' => $newSkin->toFrontend(),
                    'balance' => $request->user()->balance,
                ]);
            }

            return response()->json([
                'success' => false,
                'balance' => $request->user()->balance,
            ]);
        });
    }

    public function executeContract(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'inventory_item_ids' => ['required', 'array', 'min:2', 'max:10'],
            'inventory_item_ids.*' => ['integer', 'exists:inventory_items,id'],
        ]);

        $items = InventoryItem::with('skin')
            ->where('user_id', $request->user()->id)
            ->whereIn('id', $validated['inventory_item_ids'])
            ->get();

        if ($items->count() !== count($validated['inventory_item_ids'])) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'РќРµРєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅС‹Р№ РІС‹Р±РѕСЂ СЃРєРёРЅРѕРІ.'], 422);
        }

        $totalValue = (int) $items->sum(fn ($item) => $item->skin->price);
        $range = GameEconomy::contractRange($totalValue, $items->count(), 0);
        $minPrice = $range['min'];
        $maxPrice = $range['max'];

        $candidates = Skin::query()
            ->whereBetween('price', [$minPrice, $maxPrice])
            ->get();

        if ($candidates->isEmpty()) {
            return response()->json([
                'message' => "РќРµС‚ СЃРєРёРЅРѕРІ РІ РґРёР°РїР°Р·РѕРЅРµ {$minPrice}вЂ“{$maxPrice} в‚Ѕ РґР»СЏ РєРѕРЅС‚СЂР°РєС‚Р°.",
            ], 422);
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $items, $candidates) {
            InventoryItem::whereIn('id', $items->pluck('id'))->delete();

            $newSkin = GameEconomy::pickContractSkin($candidates);

            InventoryItem::create([
                'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
                'skin_id' => $newSkin->id,
                'source' => 'contract',
            ]);

            return response()->json([
                'skin' => $newSkin->toFrontend(),
                'balance' => $request->user()->balance,
            ]);
        });
    }

    private function userInventory(Request $request): array
    {
        if (! $request->user()) {
            return [];
        }

        return InventoryItem::with('skin')
            ->where('user_id', $request->user()->id)
            ->latest()
            ->get()
            ->map->toFrontend()
            ->all();
    }

    private function mockBattles(): array
    {
        return [
            ['id' => 1, 'players' => 2, 'maxPlayers' => 2, 'cases' => ['Phoenix Case', 'Chroma Case'], 'totalPrice' => 256, 'status' => 'waiting'],
            ['id' => 2, 'players' => 3, 'maxPlayers' => 4, 'cases' => ['Knife Case'], 'totalPrice' => 796, 'status' => 'waiting'],
            ['id' => 3, 'players' => 4, 'maxPlayers' => 4, 'cases' => ['Gamma Case', 'Spectrum Case'], 'totalPrice' => 632, 'status' => 'active'],
            ['id' => 4, 'players' => 1, 'maxPlayers' => 2, 'cases' => ['Danger Zone', 'Dreams & Nightmares'], 'totalPrice' => 216, 'status' => 'waiting'],
            ['id' => 5, 'players' => 2, 'maxPlayers' => 2, 'cases' => ['Revolution Case', 'Knife Case'], 'totalPrice' => 576, 'status' => 'active'],
        ];
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Enums\UserRole;
use App\Http\Requests\StoreUserRequest;
use App\Http\Requests\UpdateUserRequest;
use App\Models\BalanceTransaction;
use App\Models\InventoryItem;
use App\Models\SkinWithdrawal;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Models\UserSkinHistory;
use App\Services\PromoCodeService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;

class UserController extends Controller
{
    public function registerUser(StoreUserRequest $request)
    {
        $user = new User;
        $user->name = $request->name;
        $user->email = $request->email;
        $user->password = $request->password;
        $user->role = UserRole::User;
        $user->balance = 0;
        $user->save();
        $user->stat();
        Auth::login($user);

        return response()->json(['token' => $user->createToken('api')->plainTextToken]);
    }

    public function authUser(UpdateUserRequest $request)
    {
        $user = User::where('email', $request->email)->first();
        if ($user && Hash::check($request->password, $user->password)) {
            Auth::login($user);

            return response()->json(['token' => $user->createToken('api')->plainTextToken]);
        }

        return response()->json(['errors' => ['email' => ['РћС€РёР±РєР° Р°РІС‚РѕСЂРёР·Р°С†РёРё']]]);
    }

    public function logoutUser(Request $request)
    {
        $request->user()->currentAccessToken()->delete();

        return response()->json();
    }

    public function getUsers()
    {
        return User::query()
            ->orderByRaw("CASE role WHEN 'super_admin' THEN 0 WHEN 'admin' THEN 1 WHEN 'manager' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END")
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->get()
            ->map->toFrontend();
    }

    public function updateRole(Request $request, User $user)
    {
        if (! $request->user()->hasCapability('manage_roles')) {
            abort(403, 'РўРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РіР»Р°РІРЅС‹Р№ Р°РґРјРёРЅ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡Р°С‚СЊ СЂРѕР»Рё.');
        }

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'role' => ['required', Rule::in(['user', 'manager', 'admin'])],
        ]);

        if ($user->isSuperAdmin()) {
            return response()->json(['errors' => ['role' => ['РќРµР»СЊР·СЏ РёР·РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ СЂРѕР»СЊ РіР»Р°РІРЅРѕРіРѕ Р°РґРјРёРЅР°.']]], 422);
        }

        if ($user->id === $request->user()->id) {
            return response()->json(['errors' => ['role' => ['РќРµР»СЊР·СЏ РёР·РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ СЃРѕР±СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅСѓСЋ СЂРѕР»СЊ.']]], 422);
        }

        $user->update(['role' => $validated['role']]);

        return response()->json(['message' => 'Р РѕР»СЊ РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅР°']);
    }

    public function deleteUser(Request $request, User $user)
    {
        if (! $request->user()->hasCapability('delete_users')) {
            abort(403, 'РќРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ РїСЂР°РІ РґР»СЏ СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёСЏ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»РµР№.');
        }

        if ($user->id === $request->user()->id) {
            return response()->json(['errors' => ['user' => ['РќРµР»СЊР·СЏ СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СЃРІРѕР№ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚.']]], 422);
        }

        if ($user->isSuperAdmin()) {
            return response()->json(['errors' => ['user' => ['РќРµР»СЊР·СЏ СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РіР»Р°РІРЅРѕРіРѕ Р°РґРјРёРЅР°.']]], 422);
        }

        $user->delete();

        return response()->json(['message' => 'РџРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ СѓРґР°Р»С‘РЅ']);
    }

    public function updateAvatar(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'avatar' => 'required|image|max:2048',
        ]);

        $user = $request->user();

        if ($user->avatar && Storage::disk('public')->exists($user->avatar)) {
            Storage::disk('public')->delete($user->avatar);
        }

        $path = $validated['avatar']->store('avatars', 'public');
        $user->update(['avatar' => $path]);

        return response()->json([
            'avatar' => $user->fresh()->avatarUrl(),
        ]);
    }

    public function deleteAvatar(Request $request)
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        if ($user->avatar && Storage::disk('public')->exists($user->avatar)) {
            Storage::disk('public')->delete($user->avatar);
        }

        $user->update(['avatar' => null]);

        return response()->json(['avatar' => null]);
    }

    public function profile(Request $request)
    {
        $user = $request->user();
        $stats = $user->stat();

        $inventory = InventoryItem::with('skin')
            ->where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->latest()
            ->get();

        $inventoryValue = $inventory->sum(fn ($item) => $item->skin->price);

        $depositedTotal = (int) BalanceTransaction::query()
            ->where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->where('type', 'deposit')
            ->sum('amount');

        $spentOnCases = (int) abs(BalanceTransaction::query()
            ->where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->where('type', 'case_open')
            ->sum('amount'));

        $skinHistory = UserSkinHistory::query()
            ->with('skin')
            ->where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->latest()
            ->limit(24)
            ->get()
            ->map->toFrontend();

        $transactions = BalanceTransaction::query()
            ->where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->latest()
            ->limit(15)
            ->get()
            ->map(fn (BalanceTransaction $tx) => [
                'id' => $tx->id,
                'amount' => $tx->amount,
                'type' => $tx->type,
                'description' => $tx->description,
                'createdAt' => $tx->created_at->toISOString(),
            ]);

        $upgradeRate = $stats->upgrades_count > 0
            ? (int) round(($stats->upgrades_success / $stats->upgrades_count) * 100)
            : 0;

        $skinWithdrawals = SkinWithdrawal::query()
            ->where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->latest()
            ->limit(20)
            ->get()
            ->map->toFrontend();

        $promoCodeService = app(PromoCodeService::class);

        return response()->json([
            'user' => $user->toFrontend(),
            'stats' => [
                'casesOpened' => $stats->cases_opened,
                'upgradesCount' => $stats->upgrades_count,
                'upgradesSuccess' => $stats->upgrades_success,
                'upgradeRate' => $upgradeRate,
                'contractsCount' => $stats->contracts_count,
                'depositedTotal' => $depositedTotal,
                'withdrawTotal' => $stats->withdraw_total,
                'spentOnCases' => $spentOnCases,
                'inventoryCount' => $inventory->count(),
                'inventoryValue' => $inventoryValue,
                'registeredAt' => $user->created_at?->toISOString(),
            ],
            'inventory' => $inventory->map->toFrontend(),
            'skinHistory' => $skinHistory,
            'transactions' => $transactions,
            'skinWithdrawals' => $skinWithdrawals,
            'promos' => $promoCodeService->getPublicPromos(),
        ]);
    }

    public function publicProfile(User $user)
    {
        $stats = $user->stat();

        $upgradeRate = $stats->upgrades_count > 0
            ? (int) round(($stats->upgrades_success / $stats->upgrades_count) * 100)
            : 0;

        $skinHistory = UserSkinHistory::query()
            ->with('skin')
            ->where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->latest()
            ->limit(24)
            ->get()
            ->map->toFrontend();

        return response()->json([
            'user' => $user->toPublicFrontend(),
            'stats' => [
                'casesOpened' => $stats->cases_opened,
                'upgradesCount' => $stats->upgrades_count,
                'upgradeRate' => $upgradeRate,
                'contractsCount' => $stats->contracts_count,
                'withdrawTotal' => $stats->withdraw_total,
                'registeredAt' => $user->created_at?->toISOString(),
            ],
            'skinHistory' => $skinHistory,
        ]);
    }
}

    methods: ["get","head"],
    url: '/admin/cases/{gameCase}/edit',
} satisfies RouteDefinition<["get","head"]>

/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\Admin\CaseController::edit
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CaseController.php:44
 * @route '/admin/cases/{gameCase}/edit'
 */
edit.url = (args: { gameCase: number | { id: number } } | [gameCase: number | { id: number } ] | number | { id: number }, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { gameCase: args }
    }

            if (typeof args === 'object' && !Array.isArray(args) && 'id' in args) {
            args = { gameCase: args.id }
        }
    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    gameCase: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        gameCase: typeof args.gameCase === 'object'
                ? args.gameCase.id
                : args.gameCase,
                }

    return edit.definition.url
            .replace('{gameCase}', parsedArgs.gameCase.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

 */
export const edit = (args: { skin: number | { id: number } } | [skin: number | { id: number } ] | number | { id: number }, options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteDefinition<'get'> => ({
    url: edit.url(args, options),
    method: 'get',
})

edit.definition = {
    methods: ["get","head"],
    url: '/admin/skins/{skin}/edit',
} satisfies RouteDefinition<["get","head"]>

/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\Admin\SkinController::edit
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SkinController.php:38
 * @route '/admin/skins/{skin}/edit'
 */
edit.url = (args: { skin: number | { id: number } } | [skin: number | { id: number } ] | number | { id: number }, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { skin: args }
    }

            if (typeof args === 'object' && !Array.isArray(args) && 'id' in args) {
            args = { skin: args.id }
        }
    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    skin: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        skin: typeof args.skin === 'object'
                ? args.skin.id
                : args.skin,
                }

    return edit.definition.url
            .replace('{skin}', parsedArgs.skin.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\Manager\CaseController::edit
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/Manager/CaseController.php:25
 * @route '/manager/cases/{gameCase}/edit'
 */
export const edit = (args: { gameCase: number | { id: number } } | [gameCase: number | { id: number } ] | number | { id: number }, options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteDefinition<'get'> => ({
    url: edit.url(args, options),
    method: 'get',
})

edit.definition = {
    methods: ["get","head"],
    url: '/manager/cases/{gameCase}/edit',
} satisfies RouteDefinition<["get","head"]>

/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\Manager\CaseController::edit
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/Manager/CaseController.php:25
 * @route '/manager/cases/{gameCase}/edit'
 */
edit.url = (args: { gameCase: number | { id: number } } | [gameCase: number | { id: number } ] | number | { id: number }, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { gameCase: args }
    }

            if (typeof args === 'object' && !Array.isArray(args) && 'id' in args) {
            args = { gameCase: args.id }
        }
    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    gameCase: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        gameCase: typeof args.gameCase === 'object'
                ? args.gameCase.id
                : args.gameCase,
                }

    return edit.definition.url
            .replace('{gameCase}', parsedArgs.gameCase.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { gameCase: args }
    }

            if (typeof args === 'object' && !Array.isArray(args) && 'id' in args) {
            args = { gameCase: args.id }

                        gameCase: typeof args.gameCase === 'object'

import { queryParams, type RouteQueryOptions, type RouteDefinition, type RouteFormDefinition, applyUrlDefaults } from './../../../../wayfinder'
/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\CaseOpenController::open
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/CaseOpenController.php:14
 * @route '/case/{id}/open'
 */
export const open = (args: { id: string | number } | [id: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteDefinition<'post'> => ({
    url: open.url(args, options),
    method: 'post',
})

open.definition = {
    methods: ["post"],
    url: '/case/{id}/open',
} satisfies RouteDefinition<["post"]>

/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\CaseOpenController::open
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/CaseOpenController.php:14
 * @route '/case/{id}/open'
 */
open.url = (args: { id: string | number } | [id: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { id: args }
    }

    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    id: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        id: args.id,
                }

    return open.definition.url
            .replace('{id}', parsedArgs.id.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\CaseOpenController::open
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/CaseOpenController.php:14
 * @route '/case/{id}/open'
 */
open.post = (args: { id: string | number } | [id: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteDefinition<'post'> => ({
    url: open.url(args, options),
    method: 'post',
})

    /**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\CaseOpenController::open
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/CaseOpenController.php:14
 * @route '/case/{id}/open'
 */
    const openForm = (args: { id: string | number } | [id: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteFormDefinition<'post'> => ({
        action: open.url(args, options),
        method: 'post',
    })

            /**

export const showCase = (args: { id: string | number } | [id: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteDefinition<'get'> => ({
    url: showCase.url(args, options),
    method: 'get',
})

showCase.definition = {
    methods: ["get","head"],
    url: '/case/{id}',
} satisfies RouteDefinition<["get","head"]>

/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\GameController::showCase
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/GameController.php:49
 * @route '/case/{id}'
 */
showCase.url = (args: { id: string | number } | [id: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { id: args }
    }

    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    id: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        id: args.id,
                }

    return showCase.definition.url
            .replace('{id}', parsedArgs.id.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

import { queryParams, type RouteQueryOptions, type RouteDefinition, type RouteFormDefinition, applyUrlDefaults } from './../../../../../wayfinder'
/**
* @see \Laravel\Fortify\Http\Controllers\NewPasswordController::create
 * @see vendor/laravel/fortify/src/Http/Controllers/NewPasswordController.php:44
 * @route '/reset-password/{token}'
 */
export const create = (args: { token: string | number } | [token: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteDefinition<'get'> => ({
    url: create.url(args, options),
    method: 'get',
})

create.definition = {
    methods: ["get","head"],
    url: '/reset-password/{token}',
} satisfies RouteDefinition<["get","head"]>

/**
* @see \Laravel\Fortify\Http\Controllers\NewPasswordController::create
 * @see vendor/laravel/fortify/src/Http/Controllers/NewPasswordController.php:44
 * @route '/reset-password/{token}'
 */
create.url = (args: { token: string | number } | [token: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { token: args }
    }

    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    token: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        token: args.token,
                }

    return create.definition.url
            .replace('{token}', parsedArgs.token.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

 * @see vendor/laravel/fortify/src/Http/Controllers/TwoFactorAuthenticationController.php:35
 * @route '/user/two-factor-authentication'
 */
    const destroyForm = (options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteFormDefinition<'post'> => ({
        action: destroy.url({
                    [options?.mergeQuery ? 'mergeQuery' : 'query']: {
                        _method: 'DELETE',
                        ...(options?.query ?? options?.mergeQuery ?? {}),
                    }
                }),
        method: 'post',
    })

        destroyForm.delete = (options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteFormDefinition<'post'> => ({

                        [options?.mergeQuery ? 'mergeQuery' : 'query']: {
                            _method: 'DELETE',
                            ...(options?.query ?? options?.mergeQuery ?? {}),
                        }

=== FILE: C:\Users\Виталя\Desktop\лудомания — копия\resources\js\actions\Laravel\Fortify\Http\Controllers\VerifyEmailController.ts ===
import { queryParams, type RouteQueryOptions, type RouteDefinition, type RouteFormDefinition, applyUrlDefaults } from './../../../../../wayfinder'
/**
* @see \Laravel\Fortify\Http\Controllers\VerifyEmailController::__invoke
 * @see vendor/laravel/fortify/src/Http/Controllers/VerifyEmailController.php:18
 * @route '/email/verify/{id}/{hash}'
 */
export const __invoke = (args: { id: string | number, hash: string | number } | [id: string | number, hash: string | number ], options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteDefinition<'get'> => ({
    url: __invoke.url(args, options),
    method: 'get',
})

__invoke.definition = {
    methods: ["get","head"],
    url: '/email/verify/{id}/{hash}',
} satisfies RouteDefinition<["get","head"]>

/**
* @see \Laravel\Fortify\Http\Controllers\VerifyEmailController::__invoke
 * @see vendor/laravel/fortify/src/Http/Controllers/VerifyEmailController.php:18
 * @route '/email/verify/{id}/{hash}'
 */
__invoke.url = (args: { id: string | number, hash: string | number } | [id: string | number, hash: string | number ], options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    id: args[0],
                    hash: args[1],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        id: args.id,
                                hash: args.hash,
                }

    return __invoke.definition.url
            .replace('{id}', parsedArgs.id.toString())
            .replace('{hash}', parsedArgs.hash.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

/**

 * @see vendor/laravel/fortify/src/Http/Controllers/VerifyEmailController.php:18

 */

    url: __invoke.url(args, options),
    method: 'get',
})

<script setup lang="ts">
import DepositModal from '@/components/DepositModal.vue';
import { formatPrice } from '@/lib/game';
import { Link, router, usePage } from '@inertiajs/vue3';
import { Coins, LogIn, LogOut, Menu, Shield, Users, X } from 'lucide-vue-next';
import { computed, ref } from 'vue';

const page = usePage();
const mobileMenuOpen = ref(false);
const depositOpen = ref(false);

interface AuthUser {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    balance: number;
    role: string;
    roleLabel: string;
}

const user = computed(() => (page.props.auth as { user?: AuthUser })?.user ?? null);
const isLoggedIn = computed(() => !!user.value);
const isAdmin = computed(() => user.value?.role === 'admin');
const isManager = computed(() => user.value?.role === 'manager' || isAdmin.value);

const navItems = [
    { label: 'РљРµР№СЃС‹', href: '/', route: 'home' },
    { label: 'Р‘РёС‚РІС‹', href: '/battles', route: 'battles' },
    { label: 'РђРїРіСЂРµР№Рґ', href: '/upgrade', route: 'upgrade' },
    { label: 'РљРѕРЅС‚СЂР°РєС‚С‹', href: '/contracts', route: 'contracts' },
];

const currentRoute = computed(() => {
    const url = page.url;
    if (url.startsWith('/battles')) return 'battles';
    if (url.startsWith('/upgrade')) return 'upgrade';
    if (url.startsWith('/contracts')) return 'contracts';
    if (url.startsWith('/case')) return 'home';
    return 'home';
});

function logout() {
    router.post('/logout');
}
</script>

<template>
    <header class="sticky top-0 z-50 border-b border-cb-border bg-cb-bg-secondary/95 backdrop-blur-md">

                    :class="
                        currentRoute === item.route
                            ? 'bg-cb-orange/10 text-cb-orange'

                        РђРґРјРёРЅ

                    <Link

                        @click="depositOpen = true"

<template>
    <Teleport to="body">
        <div v-if="show" class="ludo-modal-backdrop" @click.self="$emit('close')">
            <div class="ludo-modal">
                <div class="ludo-modal-header">
                    <h2 class="h5 mb-0">РџРѕРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРёРµ Р±Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃР°</h2>
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-ludo-outline" @click="$emit('close')">Г—</button>
                </div>
                <div class="p-4">
                    <div class="ludo-stat-card text-center mb-3">
                        <div class="small text-secondary">РўРµРєСѓС‰РёР№ Р±Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃ</div>
                        <div class="ludo-case-price fs-3">{{ balance }} в‚Ѕ</div>
                    </div>

                    <div v-if="promos" class="ludo-promo-box mb-3">
                        <div class="small text-secondary mb-2">РђРєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ РїСЂРѕРјРѕРєРѕРґ</div>
                        <div class="ludo-promo-row">
                            <span class="ludo-promo-code">{{ promos.rotating.code }}</span>
                            <span class="ludo-promo-bonus">+{{ promos.rotating.bonusPercent }}%</span>
                            <span class="small text-secondary">{{ promoTimer }}СЃ</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <label class="form-label">РЎСѓРјРјР°</label>
                    <input v-model.number="amount" type="number" min="1" class="form-control ludo-form-control mb-3" />

                    <label class="form-label">РџСЂРѕРјРѕРєРѕРґ <span class="text-secondary fw-normal">(РЅРµРѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ)</span></label>
                    <input v-model="promoCode" type="text" class="form-control ludo-form-control mb-2 text-uppercase" placeholder="РњРѕР¶РЅРѕ РѕСЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рј" />
                    <div v-if="previewBonus > 0" class="small text-success mb-3">
                        Р‘РѕРЅСѓСЃ: +{{ previewBonus }} в‚Ѕ В· Р—Р°С‡РёСЃР»РёС‚СЃСЏ: {{ amount + previewBonus }} в‚Ѕ
                    </div>
                    <div v-if="promoError" class="ludo-alert ludo-alert-danger py-2 small mb-3">{{ promoError }}</div>

                    <div class="d-flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-4">
                        <button
                            v-for="p in presets"
                            :key="p"
                            class="btn btn-sm"
                            :class="amount === p ? 'btn-ludo-primary' : 'btn-ludo-outline'"
                            @click="amount = p"
                        >
                            {{ p }} в‚Ѕ
                        </button>
                    </div>
                    <button class="btn btn-ludo-primary w-100" :disabled="loading || amount < 1" @click="submit">
                        {{ loading ? 'РџРѕРїРѕР»РЅСЏРµРј...' : 'РџРѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚СЊ ' + amount + ' в‚Ѕ' }}
                    </button>
                    <p class="small text-secondary text-center mt-3 mb-0">Р”РµРјРѕ: СЃСЂРµРґСЃС‚РІР° Р·Р°С‡РёСЃР»СЏСЋС‚СЃСЏ РјРіРЅРѕРІРµРЅРЅРѕ</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </Teleport>
</template>

<script>
import { sounds } from '../helpers/sounds.js';

export default {
    name: 'DepositModal',
    props: ['show', 'balance', 'datasend', 'promos'],
    emits: ['close', 'done'],
    data() {
        return {
            amount: 1000,
            promoCode: '',
            loading: false,
            promoError: null,
            promoTimer: 0,
            promoInterval: null,
        };
    },
    computed: {
        previewBonus() {
            if (!this.promos || !this.promoCode.trim()) {
                return 0;
            }
            const code = this.promoCode.trim().toUpperCase();
            let percent = 0;
            if (code === 'LUDOLEGEND') {
                percent = 777;
            } else if (this.promos && code === this.promos.rotating.code) {
                percent = this.promos.rotating.bonusPercent;
            }
            if (!percent) {
                return 0;
            }
            return Math.round(this.amount * (percent / 100));
        },
    },
    watch: {
        show(val) {
            if (val) {
                this.amount = 1000;
                this.promoCode = '';
                this.promoError = null;
                this.startPromoTimer();
            } else {
                this.stopPromoTimer();
            }
        },
        promos() {
            this.syncPromoTimer();
        },
    },
    unmounted() {
        this.stopPromoTimer();
    },
    methods: {
        startPromoTimer() {
            this.syncPromoTimer();
            this.stopPromoTimer();
            this.promoInterval = setInterval(() => {
                this.syncPromoTimer();
            }, 1000);
        },
        stopPromoTimer() {
            clearInterval(this.promoInterval);
            this.promoInterval = null;
        },
        syncPromoTimer() {
            if (!this.promos || !this.promos.rotating || !this.promos.rotating.expiresAt) {
                return;
            }
            this.promoTimer = Math.max(0, this.promos.rotating.expiresAt - Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
        },
        submit() {
            if (this.loading || this.amount < 1) {
                return;
            }
            this.loading = true;
            this.promoError = null;
            sounds.click();
            let fd = new FormData();
            fd.append('amount', this.amount);
            if (this.promoCode.trim()) {
                fd.append('promo_code', this.promoCode.trim().toUpperCase());
            }
            this.datasend('balance/deposit', 'POST', fd).then((result) => {
                this.loading = false;
                if (!result) {
                    return;
                }
                if (result.message) {
                    this.promoError = result.message;
                    return;
                }
                this.$emit('done');
                this.$emit('close');
            });
        },
    },
};
</script>

<template>
    <form @submit.prevent="authUser">
        <div class="mb-3">
            <label class="form-label">Email</label>
            <input v-model="email" type="text" class="form-control ludo-form-control" placeholder="you@mail.ru" />
            <div v-if="errors.email" class="ludo-alert ludo-alert-danger mt-2 mb-0 py-2 small">{{ errors.email.join('. ') }}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="mb-4">
            <label class="form-label">РџР°СЂРѕР»СЊ</label>
            <input v-model="password" type="password" class="form-control ludo-form-control" placeholder="вЂўвЂўвЂўвЂўвЂўвЂўвЂўвЂў" />
            <div v-if="errors.password" class="ludo-alert ludo-alert-danger mt-2 mb-0 py-2 small">{{ errors.password.join('. ') }}</div>
        </div>
        <button type="submit" class="btn btn-ludo-primary w-100">Р’РѕР№С‚Рё</button>
    </form>
</template>

<script>
export default {
    name: 'LoginComponent',
    props: ['datasend', 'changeToken', 'changePage'],
    data() {
        return { email: null, password: null, errors: {} };
    },
    methods: {
        authUser() {
            this.errors = {};
            let formdata = new FormData();
            if (this.email) formdata.append('email', this.email);
            if (this.password) formdata.append('password', this.password);
            this.datasend('login', 'POST', formdata).then((result) => {
                if (!result) return;
                if (result.errors) this.errors = result.errors;
                if (result.token) {
                    this.changeToken(result.token);
                    this.changePage('HomePage');
                }
            });
        },
    },
};
</script>

<script>
export default {
    name: 'RegisterComponent',
    props: ['datasend', 'changeToken', 'changePage'],
    data() {
        return { name: null, email: null, password: null, errors: {} };
    },
    methods: {
        registerUser() {
            this.errors = {};
            let formdata = new FormData();
            if (this.name) formdata.append('name', this.name);
            if (this.email) formdata.append('email', this.email);
            if (this.password) formdata.append('password', this.password);
            this.datasend('register', 'POST', formdata).then((result) => {
                if (!result) return;
                if (result.errors) this.errors = result.errors;
                if (result.token) {
                    this.changeToken(result.token);
                    this.changePage('HomePage');
                }
            });
        },
    },
};
</script>

<template>
    <div class="ludo-user-avatar" :class="'ludo-user-avatar-' + size" :title="name || ''">
        <img v-if="avatar" :src="avatar" :alt="name" />
        <span v-else class="ludo-user-avatar-initials">{{ displayInitials }}</span>
    </div>
</template>

<script>
export default {
    name: 'UserAvatar',
    props: {
        avatar: { type: String, default: null },
        name: { type: String, default: '' },
        initials: { type: String, default: '' },
        size: { type: String, default: 'md' },
    },
    computed: {
        displayInitials() {
            if (this.initials) {
                return this.initials;
            }
            if (!this.name) {
                return '??';
            }
            const parts = this.name.trim().split(/[_\s]+/);
            if (parts.length >= 2) {
                return (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();
            }
            return this.name.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
        },
    },
};
</script>
=== FILE: C:\Users\Виталя\Desktop\лудомания — копия\resources\js\composables\useLiveFeed.ts ===
import type { LiveDrop } from '@/types';
import { echo } from '@/echo';
import { onMounted, onUnmounted, ref, watch, type Ref } from 'vue';

export function useLiveFeed(source: LiveDrop[] | Ref<LiveDrop[]>) {
    const sourceRef = Array.isArray(source) ? ref(source) : source;
    const drops = ref<LiveDrop[]>([...sourceRef.value]);

    watch(
        sourceRef,
        (newDrops) => {
            drops.value = [...newDrops];
        },
        { deep: true },
    );

    function prependDrop(drop: LiveDrop) {
        drops.value = [drop, ...drops.value.filter((d) => d.id !== drop.id)].slice(0, 50);
    }

    onMounted(() => {
        if (!echo) {
            return;
        }

        echo.channel('live-drops').listen('.LiveDropCreated', (payload: { drop: LiveDrop }) => {
            prependDrop(payload.drop);
        });
    });

    onUnmounted(() => {
        echo?.leave('live-drops');
    });

    return { drops, prependDrop };
}
