import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';

export function fireWinConfetti() {
    const colors = ['#f0b429', '#8b5cf6', '#ff6b35', '#22c55e'];
    confetti({
        particleCount: 80,
        spread: 70,
        origin: { y: 0.6 },
        colors,
    });
    setTimeout(() => {
        confetti({
            particleCount: 50,
            angle: 60,
            spread: 55,
            origin: { x: 0 },
            colors,
        });
        confetti({
            particleCount: 50,
            angle: 120,
            spread: 55,
            origin: { x: 1 },
            colors,
        });
    }, 200);
}

export function fireUpgradeConfetti(success) {
    if (!success) return;
    confetti({
        particleCount: 40,
        spread: 50,
        origin: { y: 0.65 },
        colors: ['#f0b429', '#a78bfa'],
    });
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\Виталя\Desktop\лудомания — копия\resources\js\helpers\game.js ===
export const rarityColors = {
    consumer: '#b0c3d9',
    industrial: '#5e98d9',
    'mil-spec': '#4b69ff',
    restricted: '#8847ff',
    classified: '#d32ce6',
    covert: '#eb4b4b',
    gold: '#f0b429',
};

export function formatPrice(value) {
    return Number(value).toLocaleString('ru-RU') + ' в‚Ѕ';
}

export const UPGRADE_MIN_CHANCE = 1;
export const UPGRADE_MAX_CHANCE = 75;
export const UPGRADE_BASE_MAX = 75;
export const UPGRADE_HOUSE_EDGE = 0.97;

export const CONTRACT_MIN_ITEMS = 2;
export const CONTRACT_MAX_ITEMS = 10;
export const CONTRACT_BASE_MULTIPLIER = 1.25;

export function isUpgradeTargetAllowed(skinPrice, targetPrice) {
    const source = Number(skinPrice);
    const target = Number(targetPrice);
    if (!source || !target || target <= source) {
        return false;
    }

    return (source / target) * 100 <= UPGRADE_BASE_MAX;
}

export function calcUpgradeEffectiveValue(skinPrice, boostAmount = 0) {
    return Math.max(0, Number(skinPrice) + Number(boostAmount));
}

export function calcUpgradeRawChance(skinPrice, targetPrice, boostAmount = 0) {
    if (!skinPrice || !targetPrice) {
        return 0;
    }

    const effectiveValue = calcUpgradeEffectiveValue(skinPrice, boostAmount);

    return (effectiveValue / targetPrice) * 100 * UPGRADE_HOUSE_EDGE;
}

export function calcUpgradeBaseChance(skinPrice, targetPrice) {
    return calcUpgradeChance(skinPrice, targetPrice, 0);
}

export function calcUpgradeChance(skinPrice, targetPrice, boostAmount = 0) {
    if (!skinPrice || !targetPrice) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (!isUpgradeTargetAllowed(skinPrice, targetPrice)) {
        return 0;
    }

    const chance = Math.round(calcUpgradeRawChance(skinPrice, targetPrice, boostAmount));

    return Math.min(UPGRADE_MAX_CHANCE, Math.max(UPGRADE_MIN_CHANCE, chance));
}

export function calcMaxUpgradeBoost(skinPrice, targetPrice, balance = 0) {
    const normalizedBalance = Number(balance);
    if (!skinPrice || !targetPrice || !Number.isFinite(normalizedBalance) || normalizedBalance <= 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (!isUpgradeTargetAllowed(skinPrice, targetPrice)) {
        return 0;
    }

    const targetEffective = Math.round((targetPrice * (UPGRADE_MAX_CHANCE / 100)) / UPGRADE_HOUSE_EDGE);
    const maxByChance = Math.max(0, targetEffective - Number(skinPrice));
    const maxByTarget = Math.round(Number(targetPrice) * 0.5);

    return Math.min(normalizedBalance, maxByChance, maxByTarget);
}

export function calcUpgradeBoostStep(skinPrice, targetPrice) {
    const target = Number(targetPrice);
    if (target >= 100000) {
        return 1000;
    }
    if (target >= 20000) {
        return 500;
    }
    if (target >= 5000) {
        return 100;
    }

    return 50;
}

export function calcContractRange(totalValue, itemCount, boostAmount = 0) {
    const boostFactor = Math.min(1, boostAmount / 10000);
    const multiplier = CONTRACT_BASE_MULTIPLIER + boostFactor * 1.25;
    const min = Math.max(1, Math.round(totalValue / multiplier));
    const max = Math.max(min, Math.round(totalValue * multiplier));

    return {
        min,
        max,
        avg: Math.round(totalValue / Math.max(1, itemCount)),
        total: totalValue,
        multiplier: Math.round(multiplier * 100) / 100,
    };
}

export function calcMaxContractBoost(balance = 0) {
    if (balance <= 0) {
        return 0;
    }

    return Math.min(10000, balance);
}

export const categoryOrder = ['serial', 'premium', 'image', 'branded', 'knife', 'limited', 'bonus', 'valve'];

import casePlaceholder from '../../images/case-placeholder.svg?url';
import skinPlaceholder from '../../images/skin-placeholder.svg?url';

export { rarityClass, rarityLabel } from './images-meta.js';

export const SKIN_PLACEHOLDER = skinPlaceholder;
export const CASE_PLACEHOLDER = casePlaceholder;

export function imgError(event, type = 'skin') {
    const fallback = type === 'case' ? CASE_PLACEHOLDER : SKIN_PLACEHOLDER;
    if (event.target.src !== fallback) {
        event.target.src = fallback;
    }
}

export function skinImg(src) {
    if (!src || src.includes('placeholder')) {
        return SKIN_PLACEHOLDER;
    }
    return src;
}

export function caseImg(src) {
    if (!src || src.includes('placeholder')) {
        return CASE_PLACEHOLDER;
    }
    return src;
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\Виталя\Desktop\лудомания — копия\resources\js\helpers\sounds.js ===
let audioCtx = null;

function getCtx() {
    if (!audioCtx) {
        audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    }
    return audioCtx;
}

function tone(freq, duration, type = 'sine', volume = 0.08) {
    try {
        const ctx = getCtx();
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = ctx.createGain();
        osc.type = type;
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.value = volume;
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(ctx.destination);
        osc.start();
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + duration);
        osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + duration);
    } catch {
        // audio optional
    }
}

export const sounds = {
    click: () => tone(600, 0.05, 'square', 0.04),
    spin: () => tone(220, 0.08, 'sawtooth', 0.03),
    tick: () => tone(440, 0.03, 'square', 0.025),
    win: () => {
        tone(523, 0.12, 'sine', 0.07);
        setTimeout(() => tone(659, 0.12, 'sine', 0.07), 100);
        setTimeout(() => tone(784, 0.2, 'sine', 0.08), 200);
    },
    lose: () => {
        tone(300, 0.15, 'sawtooth', 0.05);
        setTimeout(() => tone(200, 0.25, 'sawtooth', 0.04), 120);
    },
    upgrade: () => {
        tone(400, 0.1, 'triangle', 0.06);
        setTimeout(() => tone(500, 0.15, 'triangle', 0.06), 80);
    },
    contract: () => {
        tone(180, 0.2, 'square', 0.05);
        setTimeout(() => tone(350, 0.15, 'sine', 0.06), 150);
    },
};
=== FILE: C:\Users\Виталя\Desktop\лудомания — копия\resources\js\helpers\upgradeSpin.js ===
/**
 * РЈРіРѕР» СЃС‚СЂРµР»РєРё Р°РїРіСЂРµР№РґР°: Р·РµР»С‘РЅР°СЏ Р·РѕРЅР° 0вЂ¦chance% СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ РїРѕ С‡Р°СЃРѕРІРѕР№.
 *
 * @param {number} chance 1вЂ“75
 * @param {boolean} success
 * @returns {number}
 */
export function calcUpgradeArrowAngle(chance, success) {
    const clamped = Math.min(75, Math.max(1, Number(chance) || 1));
    const winSpan = (clamped / 100) * 360;
    const margin = Math.min(8, winSpan * 0.12);
    const spins = 5 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);

    let targetDeg;
    if (success) {
        const maxWin = Math.max(margin + 2, winSpan - margin);
        targetDeg = margin + Math.random() * (maxWin - margin);
    } else {
        const loseStart = Math.min(359 - margin, winSpan + margin);
        targetDeg = loseStart + Math.random() * (360 - margin - loseStart);
    }

    return spins * 360 + targetDeg;
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\Виталя\Desktop\лудомания — копия\resources\js\lib\api.ts ===
export function getCsrfToken(): string {
    return document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.getAttribute('content') ?? '';
}

export async function apiPost<T>(url: string, body: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Promise<T> {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            Accept: 'application/json',
            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': getCsrfToken(),
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });

    const data = await response.json();

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.message ?? 'РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃР°');
    }

    return data as T;
}

<script setup lang="ts">
import AdminLayout from '@/layouts/AdminLayout.vue';
import { formatPrice, rarityColors } from '@/lib/game';
import type { Skin } from '@/types';
import { Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/vue3';
import { Plus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next';
import { computed } from 'vue';

interface CaseSkin extends Skin {
    dropWeight: number;
}

interface CaseFormData {
    id?: number;
    name: string;
    price: number;
    image: string;
    category: string;
    featured: boolean;
    skins?: CaseSkin[];
}

const props = defineProps<{
    caseItem: CaseFormData | null;
    allSkins: Skin[];
    categories: { value: string; label: string }[];
}>();

const isEdit = computed(() => !!props.caseItem?.id);

const form = useForm({
    name: props.caseItem?.name ?? '',
    price: props.caseItem?.price ?? 49,
    image: props.caseItem?.image ?? '',
    category: props.caseItem?.category ?? 'popular',
    featured: props.caseItem?.featured ?? false,
    skins: (props.caseItem?.skins ?? []).map((s) => ({
        skin_id: s.id,
        drop_weight: s.dropWeight,
    })) as { skin_id: number; drop_weight: number }[],
});

function addSkin(skinId: number) {
    if (form.skins.some((s) => s.skin_id === skinId)) return;
    form.skins.push({ skin_id: skinId, drop_weight: 100 });
}

function removeSkin(index: number) {
    form.skins.splice(index, 1);
}

function getSkin(skinId: number): Skin | undefined {
    return props.allSkins.find((s) => s.id === skinId);
}

function submit() {
    if (isEdit.value && props.caseItem?.id) {
        form.put(`/admin/cases/${props.caseItem.id}`);
    } else {
        form.post('/admin/cases');
    }
}
</script>

    <AdminLayout :title="isEdit ? 'Р РµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РєРµР№СЃ' : 'РЎРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ РєРµР№СЃ'">

<script setup lang="ts">
import AdminLayout from '@/layouts/AdminLayout.vue';
import { formatPrice } from '@/lib/game';
import { Link, router } from '@inertiajs/vue3';
import { Pencil, Plus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface CaseRow {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    price: number;
    category: string;
    featured: boolean;
    image: string;
    skinsCount: number;
}

defineProps<{
    cases: CaseRow[];
}>();

<script setup lang="ts">
import AdminLayout from '@/layouts/AdminLayout.vue';
import type { Skin } from '@/types';
import { Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/vue3';
import { computed } from 'vue';

const props = defineProps<{
    skin: Skin | null;
    rarities: { value: string; label: string }[];
}>();

const isEdit = computed(() => !!props.skin);

const form = useForm({
    name: props.skin?.name ?? '',
    weapon: props.skin?.weapon ?? '',
    rarity: props.skin?.rarity ?? 'consumer',
    price: props.skin?.price ?? 100,
    image: props.skin?.image ?? '',
});

function submit() {
    if (isEdit.value && props.skin) {
        form.put(`/admin/skins/${props.skin.id}`);
    } else {
        form.post('/admin/skins');
    }
}
</script>

<template>
    <AdminLayout :title="isEdit ? 'Р РµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ СЃРєРёРЅ' : 'Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ СЃРєРёРЅ'">

import { formatPrice } from '@/lib/game';

import { Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface AdminUser {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    balance: number;
    role: string;
    roleLabel: string;
    createdAt?: string;
}

defineProps<{
    users: AdminUser[];
    roles: { value: string; label: string }[];
}>();

const roleBadgeClass: Record<string, string> = {
    admin: 'bg-cb-red/20 text-cb-red',
    manager: 'bg-cb-gold/20 text-cb-gold',
    user: 'bg-cb-border text-cb-text-muted',
};

function updateRole(userId: number, role: string) {
    router.patch(`/admin/users/${userId}/role`, { role }, { preserveScroll: true });
}

function deleteUser(userId: number, name: string) {
    if (confirm(`РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЏ ${name}?`)) {
        router.delete(`/admin/users/${userId}`, { preserveScroll: true });
    }
}

<script setup lang="ts">
import SkinItem from '@/components/SkinItem.vue';
import CaseBattleLayout from '@/layouts/CaseBattleLayout.vue';
import { formatPrice, rarityColors } from '@/lib/game';
import { apiPost } from '@/lib/api';
import type { Case, LiveDrop, Skin } from '@/types';
import { Link, router, usePage } from '@inertiajs/vue3';
import { ArrowLeft, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-vue-next';
import { computed, ref } from 'vue';

const props = defineProps<{
    caseItem: Case;
    liveDrops: LiveDrop[];
}>();

const page = usePage();
const isLoggedIn = computed(() => !!(page.props.auth as { user?: unknown })?.user);

const isOpening = ref(false);
const wonSkin = ref<Skin | null>(null);
const showResult = ref(false);
const errorMessage = ref('');
const rouletteItems = ref<Skin[]>([]);
const rouletteRef = ref<HTMLElement | null>(null);

function generateRouletteItems(winner: Skin): Skin[] {
    const items: Skin[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
        items.push(props.caseItem.items[Math.floor(Math.random() * props.caseItem.items.length)]);
    }
    items[40] = winner;
    return items;
}

function animateRoulette() {
    setTimeout(() => {
        if (rouletteRef.value) {
            const itemWidth = 140;
            const winIndex = 40;
            const offset = winIndex * itemWidth - rouletteRef.value.parentElement!.offsetWidth / 2 + itemWidth / 2;
            rouletteRef.value.style.setProperty('--spin-distance', `-${offset}px`);
            rouletteRef.value.classList.add('animate-spin-roulette');
        }
    }, 100);
}

async function openCase() {
    if (isOpening.value) return;

    if (!isLoggedIn.value) {
        router.visit('/login');
        return;
    }

    isOpening.value = true;
    showResult.value = false;
    wonSkin.value = null;
    errorMessage.value = '';

    try {
        const data = await apiPost<{ skin: Skin; balance: number }>(`/case/${props.caseItem.id}/open`);

        rouletteItems.value = generateRouletteItems(data.skin);
        animateRoulette();

        setTimeout(() => {
            wonSkin.value = data.skin;
            showResult.value = true;
            isOpening.value = false;
            if (rouletteRef.value) {
                rouletteRef.value.classList.remove('animate-spin-roulette');
            }
            router.reload({ only: ['auth'] });
        }, 4200);
    } catch (error) {
        isOpening.value = false;
        errorMessage.value = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РєРµР№СЃ';
    }
}

function reset() {
    showResult.value = false;
    wonSkin.value = null;
    rouletteItems.value = [];
    errorMessage.value = '';
}
</script>

<template>
    <CaseBattleLayout :live-drops="liveDrops">
        <section class="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 lg:px-6">
            <Link href="/" class="mb-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-cb-text-muted transition-colors hover:text-cb-text">
                <ArrowLeft class="h-4 w-4" />
                РќР°Р·Р°Рґ Рє РєРµР№СЃР°Рј
            </Link>

            <div class="mb-8 flex flex-col items-center gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:justify-center">
                <img :src="caseItem.image" :alt="caseItem.name" class="h-24 w-24 object-contain" />
                <div class="text-center sm:text-left">
                    <h1 class="font-display text-3xl font-bold">{{ caseItem.name }}</h1>
                    <p class="mt-1 font-display text-xl font-bold text-cb-gold">{{ formatPrice(caseItem.price) }}</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="relative mb-8 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-cb-border bg-cb-bg-card">
                <div class="absolute left-1/2 top-0 z-10 h-full w-0.5 -translate-x-1/2 bg-cb-orange shadow-[0_0_10px_#ff6b00]" />
                <div class="absolute left-1/2 top-0 z-10 -translate-x-1/2 border-x-[10px] border-t-[14px] border-x-transparent border-t-cb-orange" />

                <div class="overflow-hidden py-8">
                    <div v-if="rouletteItems.length === 0" class="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
                        <p class="text-cb-text-muted">РќР°Р¶РјРёС‚Рµ В«РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РєРµР№СЃВ» С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅР°С‡Р°С‚СЊ</p>
                    </div>
                    <div v-else ref="rouletteRef" class="flex gap-3 px-4" style="width: max-content">
                        <div
                            v-for="(item, index) in rouletteItems"
                            :key="index"
                            class="w-[128px] shrink-0 rounded-lg border bg-cb-bg-secondary p-2"
                            :style="{ borderColor: `${rarityColors[item.rarity]}40` }"
                        >
                            <img :src="item.image" :alt="item.name" class="mx-auto h-20 w-20 object-contain" />
                            <p class="mt-1 truncate text-center text-[10px] font-semibold">{{ item.weapon }}</p>
                            <p class="truncate text-center text-xs">{{ item.name }}</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div v-if="showResult && wonSkin" class="mb-8 flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
                <p class="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-cb-green">Р’С‹ РІС‹РёРіСЂР°Р»Рё!</p>
                <div class="w-48">
                    <SkinItem :skin="wonSkin" />
                </div>
                <button class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-cb-text-muted hover:text-cb-text" @click="reset">
                    <RotateCcw class="h-4 w-4" />
                    РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РµС‰С‘ СЂР°Р·
                </button>
            </div>

            <p v-if="errorMessage" class="mb-4 text-center text-sm text-cb-red">{{ errorMessage }}</p>

            <div v-if="!showResult" class="mb-10 flex justify-center">
                <button
                    class="animate-pulse-glow rounded-xl bg-gradient-orange px-12 py-4 font-display text-xl font-bold text-white transition-all hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-50"
                    :disabled="isOpening"
                    @click="openCase"
                >
                    {{ isOpening ? 'РћС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°РµРј...' : `РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ Р·Р° ${formatPrice(caseItem.price)}` }}
                </button>
            </div>

            <div>
                <h2 class="mb-4 font-display text-xl font-bold">РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ РєРµР№СЃР°</h2>
                <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5">
                    <SkinItem v-for="item in caseItem.items" :key="item.id" :skin="item" />
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>
    </CaseBattleLayout>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import ManagerLayout from '@/layouts/ManagerLayout.vue';
import { formatPrice, rarityColors } from '@/lib/game';
import type { Skin } from '@/types';
import { Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/vue3';
import { Plus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next';

interface CaseSkin extends Skin {
    dropWeight: number;
}

interface CaseFormData {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    price: number;
    image: string;
    category: string;
    featured: boolean;
    skins: CaseSkin[];
}

const props = defineProps<{
    caseItem: CaseFormData;
    allSkins: Skin[];
    categories: { value: string; label: string }[];
}>();

const form = useForm({
    name: props.caseItem.name,
    price: props.caseItem.price,
    image: props.caseItem.image,
    category: props.caseItem.category,
    featured: props.caseItem.featured,
    skins: props.caseItem.skins.map((s) => ({
        skin_id: s.id,
        drop_weight: s.dropWeight,
    })),
});

function addSkin(skinId: number) {
    if (form.skins.some((s) => s.skin_id === skinId)) return;
    form.skins.push({ skin_id: skinId, drop_weight: 100 });
}

function removeSkin(index: number) {
    form.skins.splice(index, 1);
}

function getSkin(skinId: number) {
    return props.allSkins.find((s) => s.id === skinId);
}

function submit() {
    form.put(`/manager/cases/${props.caseItem.id}`);
}
</script>

<template>
    <ManagerLayout title="Р РµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РєРµР№СЃ">
        <form class="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2" @submit.prevent="submit">
            <div class="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-cb-border bg-cb-bg-card p-5">
                <div>
                    <label class="mb-1 block text-sm text-cb-text-muted">РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ</label>
                    <input v-model="form.name" required class="w-full rounded-xl border border-cb-border bg-cb-bg-secondary px-4 py-2.5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label class="mb-1 block text-sm text-cb-text-muted">Р¦РµРЅР° (в‚Ѕ)</label>
                    <input v-model.number="form.price" type="number" min="1" required class="w-full rounded-xl border border-cb-border bg-cb-bg-secondary px-4 py-2.5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label class="mb-1 block text-sm text-cb-text-muted">URL РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ</label>
                    <input v-model="form.image" type="url" required class="w-full rounded-xl border border-cb-border bg-cb-bg-secondary px-4 py-2.5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label class="mb-1 block text-sm text-cb-text-muted">РљР°С‚РµРіРѕСЂРёСЏ</label>
                    <select v-model="form.category" class="w-full rounded-xl border border-cb-border bg-cb-bg-secondary px-4 py-2.5">
                        <option v-for="cat in categories" :key="cat.value" :value="cat.value">{{ cat.label }}</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
                <label class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <input v-model="form.featured" type="checkbox" class="accent-cb-orange" />

                <h2 class="font-semibold">РЎРєРёРЅС‹ РІ РєРµР№СЃРµ</h2>
                <div v-for="(entry, index) in form.skins" :key="entry.skin_id" class="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-cb-border p-3">
                    <img v-if="getSkin(entry.skin_id)" :src="getSkin(entry.skin_id)!.image" class="h-10 w-10" />
                    <div class="flex-1 truncate text-sm">{{ getSkin(entry.skin_id)?.name }}</div>
                    <input v-model.number="entry.drop_weight" type="number" min="1" class="w-16 rounded border border-cb-border px-2 py-1 text-xs" />
                    <button type="button" @click="removeSkin(index)"><Trash2 class="h-4 w-4 text-cb-red" /></button>
                </div>
                <div class="max-h-40 overflow-y-auto">
                    <button
                        v-for="skin in allSkins"
                        :key="skin.id"
                        type="button"
                        class="flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm hover:bg-cb-bg-hover disabled:opacity-40"
                        :disabled="form.skins.some((s) => s.skin_id === skin.id)"
                        @click="addSkin(skin.id)"
                    >
                        <Plus class="h-3 w-3" /> {{ skin.weapon }} | {{ skin.name }}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex gap-3 lg:col-span-2">
                <button type="submit" class="rounded-xl bg-gradient-orange px-6 py-2.5 font-semibold text-white">РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ</button>
                <Link href="/manager/cases" class="rounded-xl border border-cb-border px-6 py-2.5 text-cb-text-muted">РќР°Р·Р°Рґ</Link>
            </div>
        </form>
    </ManagerLayout>
</template>

                            <thead>
                                <tr>
                                    <th>РЎРєРёРЅ</th>

                                    <th></th>

                                        <button
                                            class="btn btn-sm btn-ludo-outline"
                                            :disabled="isSkinSelected(skin.id)"
                                            @click="addSkin(skin)"
                                        >
                                            {{ isSkinSelected(skin.id) ? 'Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅ' : '+' }}
                                        </button>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                                <tr v-if="!filteredSkins.length">
                                    <td colspan="3" class="text-secondary text-center py-3">РќРёС‡РµРіРѕ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ</td>

        <div class="ludo-table-wrap">
            <div class="table-responsive">
                <table class="table ludo-table table-striped">
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th>РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ</th>
                            <th>РљР°С‚РµРіРѕСЂРёСЏ</th>
                            <th>Р¦РµРЅР°</th>

<script>
import { categoryLabels } from '../helpers/game.js';

export default {
    name: 'AdminCasesPage',
    props: ['datasend', 'datasendJson', 'caseImg', 'imgError'],
    data() {
        return {
            cases: [],
            allSkins: [],
            selectedSkins: [],
            skinSearch: '',
            editId: null,
            saving: false,
            message: null,
            imageFile: null,
            previewImage: null,
            categoryLabels,
            form: {
                name: '',
                price: 100,
                category: 'serial',
                featured: false,
            },
        };
    },
    computed: {
        cheapTierCount() {
            if (this.form.price < 5000) {
                return 0;
            }
            const min = Math.max(500, Math.floor(this.form.price * 0.5));
            const max = Math.floor(this.form.price * 0.8);
            return this.selectedSkins.filter((item) => item.price >= min && item.price <= max).length;
        },
        filteredSkins() {
            const q = this.skinSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
            if (!q) {
                return this.allSkins.slice(0, 80);
            }
            return this.allSkins
                .filter((skin) => {
                    return (
                        skin.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
                        skin.weapon.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
                        String(skin.price).includes(q)
                    );
                })
                .slice(0, 80);
        },
    },
    mounted() {
        this.getCases();
        this.getSkins();
    },
    methods: {
        getCases() {
            this.datasend('admin/cases').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.cases = result;
                }
            });
        },
        getSkins() {
            this.datasend('admin/skins').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.allSkins = result;
                }
            });
        },
        isSkinSelected(id) {
            return this.selectedSkins.some((item) => item.skin_id === id);
        },
        addSkin(skin) {
            if (this.isSkinSelected(skin.id)) {
                return;
            }
            this.selectedSkins.push({
                skin_id: skin.id,
                drop_weight: 100,
                name: skin.name,
                weapon: skin.weapon,
                price: skin.price,
            });
        },
        removeSkin(id) {
            this.selectedSkins = this.selectedSkins.filter((item) => item.skin_id !== id);
        },
        resetForm() {
            this.editId = null;
            this.message = null;
            this.selectedSkins = [];
            this.skinSearch = '';
            this.imageFile = null;
            this.previewImage = null;
            this.form = { name: '', price: 100, category: 'serial', featured: false };
        },
        onImagePick(event) {
            const file = event.target.files && event.target.files[0];
            this.imageFile = file || null;
            this.previewImage = file ? URL.createObjectURL(file) : this.previewImage;
        },
        editCase(item) {
            this.message = null;
            this.datasend('admin/cases/' + item.id).then((result) => {
                if (!result || result.message) {
                    this.message = result?.message || 'РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ РєРµР№СЃ';
                    return;
                }
                this.editId = result.id;
                this.form = {
                    name: result.name,
                    price: result.price,
                    category: result.category,
                    featured: !!result.featured,
                };
                this.imageFile = null;
                this.previewImage = this.caseImg ? this.caseImg(result.image) : result.image;
                this.selectedSkins = (result.skins || []).map((skin) => ({
                    skin_id: skin.skin_id || skin.id,
                    drop_weight: skin.drop_weight || 100,
                    name: skin.name,
                    weapon: skin.weapon,
                    price: skin.price,
                }));
            });
        },
        save() {
            if (!this.form.name.trim()) {
                this.message = 'РЈРєР°Р¶РёС‚Рµ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ РєРµР№СЃР°';
                return;
            }
            this.saving = true;
            this.message = null;
            const route = this.editId ? 'admin/cases/' + this.editId : 'admin/cases';
            const payload = {
                ...this.form,
                skins: this.selectedSkins.map((item) => ({
                    skin_id: item.skin_id,
                    drop_weight: item.drop_weight,
                })),
            };

            this.datasendJson(route, 'POST', payload).then((result) => {
                if (!result || result.message || result.errors) {
                    this.saving = false;
                    if (!result) {
                        this.message = 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёСЏ';
                    } else if (result.message) {
                        this.message = result.message;
                    } else if (result.errors) {
                        this.message = Object.values(result.errors).flat().join(' ');
                    }
                    return;
                }

                const caseId = result.id || this.editId;
                if (this.imageFile && caseId) {
                    const fd = new FormData();
                    fd.append('image', this.imageFile);
                    this.datasend('admin/cases/' + caseId + '/image', 'POST', fd).then(() => {
                        this.saving = false;
                        this.resetForm();
                        this.getCases();
                    });
                    return;
                }

                this.saving = false;
                this.resetForm();
                this.getCases();
            });
        },
        removeCase(id) {
            if (!confirm('РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РєРµР№СЃ?')) return;
            this.datasend('admin/cases/' + id, 'DELETE').then(() => this.getCases());
        },
    },
};
</script>

<script>
export default {
    name: 'AdminOrdersPage',
    props: ['datasend', 'datasendJson'],
    data() {
        return {
            orders: [],
            statusFilter: 'all',
            filters: [
                { value: 'all', label: 'Р’СЃРµ' },
                { value: 'pending', label: 'РћР¶РёРґР°СЋС‚' },
                { value: 'processing', label: 'Р’ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Рµ' },
                { value: 'completed', label: 'Р’С‹РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅС‹' },
                { value: 'cancelled', label: 'РћС‚РјРµРЅРµРЅС‹' },
            ],
        };
    },
    computed: {
        filteredOrders() {
            if (this.statusFilter === 'all') {
                return this.orders;
            }
            return this.orders.filter((order) => order.status === this.statusFilter);
        },
    },
    mounted() {
        this.loadOrders();
    },
    methods: {
        loadOrders() {
            this.datasend('admin/withdrawals').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.orders = result;
                }
            });
        },
        canManage(order) {
            return order.status === 'pending' || order.status === 'processing';
        },
        statusClass(status) {
            return {
                pending: 'bg-warning text-dark',
                processing: 'bg-info text-dark',
                completed: 'bg-success',
                cancelled: 'bg-secondary',
            }[status] || 'bg-secondary';
        },
        formatDate(value) {
            if (!value) {
                return 'вЂ”';
            }
            return new Date(value).toLocaleString('ru-RU');
        },
        setStatus(id, status) {
            const labels = {
                processing: 'РІР·СЏС‚СЊ РІ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Сѓ',
                completed: 'РѕС‚РјРµС‚РёС‚СЊ РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРЅРѕР№',
                cancelled: 'РѕС‚РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ (СЃРєРёРЅ РІРµСЂРЅС‘С‚СЃСЏ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЋ)',
            };
            if (!confirm('РџРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂРґРёС‚СЊ: ' + labels[status] + '?')) {
                return;
            }
            this.datasendJson('admin/withdrawals/' + id + '/status', 'POST', { status: status }).then((result) => {
                if (result && result.message) {
                    alert(result.message);
                    return;
                }
                if (result) {
                    this.loadOrders();
                }
            });
        },
    },
};
</script>

                                        :value="u.role"
                                        @change="updateRole(u.id, $event.target.value)"

<script>
import UserAvatar from '../components/UserAvatar.vue';

export default {
    name: 'AdminPage',
    components: { UserAvatar },
    props: ['datasend', 'datasendJson'],
    data() {
        return {
            users: [],
            role: {
                super_admin: 'Р“Р»Р°РІРЅС‹Р№ Р°РґРјРёРЅ',
                admin: 'РђРґРјРёРЅ',
                manager: 'РњРµРЅРµРґР¶РµСЂ',
                user: 'РџРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ',

        };

        this.getUsers();

        roleBadgeClass(role) {
            if (role === 'super_admin') return 'bg-warning text-dark';
            if (role === 'admin') return 'bg-danger';
            if (role === 'manager') return 'bg-info text-dark';
            return 'bg-secondary';
        },
        getUsers() {
            this.datasend('admin/users').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.users = result;
                }
            });
        },
        updateRole(id, role) {
            this.datasendJson('admin/users/' + id + '/role', 'POST', { role }).then((result) => {
                if (result?.errors) {
                    alert(Object.values(result.errors).flat().join('\n'));
                }
                this.getUsers();
            });
        },
        removeUser(id) {
            if (!confirm('РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЏ?')) return;
            this.datasend('admin/users/' + id, 'DELETE').then(() => this.getUsers());
        },
    },
};
</script>

            <div class="row g-2">
                <div class="col-md-3"><input v-model="form.name" class="form-control ludo-form-control" placeholder="РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ" /></div>
                <div class="col-md-2"><input v-model="form.weapon" class="form-control ludo-form-control" placeholder="РћСЂСѓР¶РёРµ" /></div>
                <div class="col-md-2">
                    <select v-model="form.rarity" class="form-select ludo-form-control">
                        <option value="consumer">РЁРёСЂРїРѕС‚СЂРµР±</option>
                        <option value="industrial">РџСЂРѕРјС‹С€Р»РµРЅРЅРѕРµ РєР°С‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ</option>
                        <option value="mil-spec">РђСЂРјРµР№СЃРєРѕРµ РєР°С‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ</option>
                        <option value="restricted">Р—Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅРЅРѕРµ</option>
                        <option value="classified">Р—Р°СЃРµРєСЂРµС‡РµРЅРЅРѕРµ</option>
                        <option value="covert">РўР°Р№РЅРѕРµ</option>
                        <option value="gold">в… Р­РєР·РѕС‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРµ</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div class="col-md-2"><input v-model.number="form.price" type="number" class="form-control ludo-form-control" placeholder="Р¦РµРЅР°" /></div>
                <div class="col-md-3">
                    <label class="btn btn-sm btn-ludo-outline w-100 mb-0">
                        {{ imageFile ? 'Р¤Р°Р№Р» РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅ' : 'РљР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєР° (РЅРµРѕР±СЏР·.)' }}
                        <input type="file" accept="image/*" class="d-none" @change="onImagePick" />
                    </label>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div v-if="previewImage" class="mt-3 text-center">
                <img :src="previewImage" style="height: 72px" class="mb-2" @error="imgError($event, 'skin')" />
            </div>
            <div v-if="message" class="ludo-alert ludo-alert-danger mt-3 mb-0">{{ message }}</div>
            <button class="btn btn-ludo-primary btn-sm mt-3" :disabled="saving" @click="save">
                {{ saving ? '...' : editId ? 'РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ' : 'Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ' }}
            </button>
        </div>

        <div class="row g-3">
            <div v-for="s in skins" :key="s.id" class="col-md-3">
                <div class="ludo-card p-3 text-center">
                    <img :src="skinImg(s.image)" style="height: 60px" class="mb-2" @error="imgError($event, 'skin')" />
                    <div class="small">{{ s.weapon }} | {{ s.name }}</div>
                    <div class="ludo-case-price mb-2">{{ s.price }} в‚Ѕ</div>
                    <button class="btn btn-ludo-outline btn-sm me-1" @click="editSkin(s)">вњЋ</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" @click="removeSkin(s.id)">Г—</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</template>

<script>
export default {
    name: 'AdminSkinsPage',
    props: ['datasend', 'datasendJson', 'skinImg', 'imgError'],
    data() {
        return {
            skins: [],
            editId: null,
            saving: false,
            message: null,
            imageFile: null,
            previewImage: null,
            form: { name: '', weapon: '', rarity: 'consumer', price: 100 },
        };
    },
    mounted() {
        this.getSkins();
    },
    methods: {
        getSkins() {
            this.datasend('admin/skins').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.skins = result;
                }
            });
        },
        onImagePick(event) {
            const file = event.target.files && event.target.files[0];
            this.imageFile = file || null;
            this.previewImage = file ? URL.createObjectURL(file) : this.previewImage;
        },
        resetForm() {
            this.editId = null;
            this.message = null;
            this.imageFile = null;
            this.previewImage = null;
            this.form = { name: '', weapon: '', rarity: 'consumer', price: 100 };
        },
        save() {
            this.saving = true;
            this.message = null;
            const route = this.editId ? 'admin/skins/' + this.editId : 'admin/skins';

            const done = (result) => {
                this.saving = false;
                if (!result) {
                    this.message = 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёСЏ';
                    return;
                }
                if (result.message && !result.id) {
                    this.message = result.message;
                    return;
                }
                if (result.errors) {
                    this.message = Object.values(result.errors).flat().join(' ');
                    return;
                }
                this.resetForm();
                this.getSkins();
            };

            if (this.imageFile) {
                const fd = new FormData();
                fd.append('name', this.form.name);
                fd.append('weapon', this.form.weapon);
                fd.append('rarity', this.form.rarity);
                fd.append('price', String(this.form.price));
                fd.append('image', this.imageFile);
                this.datasend(route, 'POST', fd).then(done);
            } else {
                this.datasendJson(route, 'POST', this.form).then(done);
            }
        },
        editSkin(s) {
            this.editId = s.id;
            this.imageFile = null;
            this.previewImage = this.skinImg(s.image);
            this.form = { name: s.name, weapon: s.weapon, rarity: s.rarity, price: s.price };
        },
        removeSkin(id) {
            if (!confirm('РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ?')) {
                return;
            }
            this.datasend('admin/skins/' + id, 'DELETE').then(() => {
                this.getSkins();
            });
        },
    },
};
</script>

                                <div class="ludo-case-price small">{{ item.price }} в‚Ѕ</div>
                                <span class="ludo-rarity-badge">{{ rarityLabel[item.rarity] }}</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

<script>
import SkinItem from '../components/SkinItem.vue';
import { fireWinConfetti } from '../helpers/confetti.js';
import { sounds } from '../helpers/sounds.js';

const ITEM_WIDTH = 132;
const ITEM_WIDTH_MULTI = 108;
const SPIN_MS = 5200;

export default {
    name: 'CasePage',
    components: { SkinItem },
    props: ['changePage', 'datasend', 'pageId', 'user', 'userInfo', 'getUser', 'onLiveDrop', 'rarityClass', 'rarityLabel', 'skinImg', 'caseImg', 'imgError'],
    data() {
        return {
            caseItem: null,
            wonSkins: [],
            openCount: 1,
            loading: false,
            spinning: false,
            message: null,
            rouletteVisible: false,
            roulettes: [],
            fastOpen: false,
            tickTimer: null,
            spinTimer: null,
            sellingIds: [],
        };
    },
    computed: {
        totalPrice() {
            if (!this.caseItem) {
                return 0;
            }
            return this.caseItem.price * this.openCount;
        },
        openButtonLabel() {
            if (this.openCount === 1) {
                return 'РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РєРµР№СЃ';
            }
            return 'РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ x' + this.openCount;
        },
    },
    mounted() {
        this.getCase();
    },
    unmounted() {
        this.clearSpinTimers();
    },
    methods: {
        getCase() {
            this.datasend('cases/' + this.pageId).then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.caseItem = result;
                    this.clampOpenCount();
                }
            });
        },
        canAffordCount(count) {
            if (!this.caseItem || this.userInfo.balance === undefined) {
                return false;
            }
            return this.userInfo.balance >= this.caseItem.price * count;
        },
        clampOpenCount() {
            if (!this.canAffordCount(this.openCount)) {
                for (let n = 5; n >= 1; n--) {
                    if (this.canAffordCount(n)) {
                        this.openCount = n;
                        return;
                    }
                }
                this.openCount = 1;
            }
        },
        setOpenCount(n) {
            if (!this.canAffordCount(n) || this.loading) {
                return;
            }
            sounds.click();
            this.openCount = n;
        },
        clearSpinTimers() {
            clearInterval(this.tickTimer);
            clearTimeout(this.spinTimer);
            this.tickTimer = null;
            this.spinTimer = null;
        },
        resetOpen() {
            this.wonSkins = [];
            this.message = null;
            this.rouletteVisible = false;
            this.roulettes = [];
            this.clearSpinTimers();
        },
        getSkinsFromResult(result) {
            if (result.skins && result.skins.length) {
                return result.skins;
            }
            if (result.skin) {
                return [result.skin];
            }
            return [];
        },
        buildStripWithWinner(winner, winIndex) {
            const pool = this.caseItem.items || [];
            const strip = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < winIndex + 8; i++) {
                strip.push(i === winIndex ? winner : pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)]);
            }
            return strip;
        },
        getItemWidth(count) {
            return count > 1 ? ITEM_WIDTH_MULTI : ITEM_WIDTH;
        },
        calcOffset(winIndex, itemWidth) {
            const viewport = Math.min(window.innerWidth - 48, 960);
            return -(winIndex * itemWidth - (viewport / 2 - itemWidth / 2));
        },
        finishOpen(result) {
            const skins = this.getSkinsFromResult(result);
            this.wonSkins = skins;
            this.getUser();
            sounds.win();
            fireWinConfetti();

            const drops = result.liveDrops || (result.liveDrop ? [result.liveDrop] : []);
            if (this.onLiveDrop) {
                for (let i = 0; i < drops.length; i++) {
                    this.onLiveDrop(drops[i]);
                }
            }
        },
        startRouletteAnimation(skins, result) {
            const winIndex = 38;
            const itemWidth = this.getItemWidth(skins.length);

            this.roulettes = skins.map(function (skin) {
                return {
                    strip: this.buildStripWithWinner(skin, winIndex),
                    offset: 0,
                    transition: 'none',
                };
            }, this);

            this.rouletteVisible = true;
            this.clearSpinTimers();

            this.tickTimer = setInterval(function () {
                sounds.tick();
            }, 120);

            this.$nextTick(() => {
                requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                    const targetOffset = this.calcOffset(winIndex, itemWidth);
                    this.roulettes.forEach(function (roulette) {
                        roulette.transition = 'transform 5s cubic-bezier(0.12, 0.75, 0.18, 1)';
                        roulette.offset = targetOffset;
                    });

                    this.spinTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                        this.clearSpinTimers();
                        this.loading = false;
                        this.spinning = false;
                        this.finishOpen(result);
                    }, SPIN_MS);
                });
            });
        },
        openCase() {
            if (!this.caseItem.items || !this.caseItem.items.length) {
                return;
            }
            if (!this.canAffordCount(this.openCount)) {
                this.message = 'РќРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ СЃСЂРµРґСЃС‚РІ РЅР° Р±Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃРµ.';
                sounds.lose();
                return;
            }

            sounds.click();
            this.loading = true;
            this.spinning = true;
            this.message = null;
            this.wonSkins = [];
            this.rouletteVisible = false;
            this.roulettes = [];

            let formdata = new FormData();
            formdata.append('count', this.openCount);

            this.datasend('cases/' + this.pageId + '/open', 'POST', formdata).then((result) => {
                if (!result) {
                    this.loading = false;
                    this.spinning = false;
                    return;
                }
                if (result.message) {
                    this.message = result.message;
                    this.loading = false;
                    this.spinning = false;
                    sounds.lose();
                    this.clampOpenCount();
                    return;
                }

                const skins = this.getSkinsFromResult(result);
                if (!skins.length) {
                    this.loading = false;
                    this.spinning = false;
                    return;
                }

                if (this.fastOpen) {
                    this.rouletteVisible = false;
                    this.loading = false;
                    this.spinning = false;
                    this.finishOpen(result);
                    return;
                }

                this.startRouletteAnimation(skins, result);
            });
        },
        sellWonSkin(skin) {
            if (!skin || !skin.inventory_item_id) {
                return;
            }
            if (!confirm('РџСЂРѕРґР°С‚СЊ РїСЂРµРґРјРµС‚ Р·Р° ' + skin.price + ' в‚Ѕ?')) {
                return;
            }
            if (!this.sellingIds.includes(skin.inventory_item_id)) {
                this.sellingIds.push(skin.inventory_item_id);
            }
            this.datasend('inventory/' + skin.inventory_item_id + '/sell', 'POST', new FormData()).then((result) => {
                const idx = this.sellingIds.indexOf(skin.inventory_item_id);
                if (idx >= 0) this.sellingIds.splice(idx, 1);
                if (!result || result.message) {
                    return;
                }
                this.wonSkins = this.wonSkins.filter((item) => item.inventory_item_id !== skin.inventory_item_id);
                this.getUser();
            });
        },
    },
};
</script>

<script setup lang="ts">
import SkinItem from '@/components/SkinItem.vue';
import CaseBattleLayout from '@/layouts/CaseBattleLayout.vue';
import { apiPost } from '@/lib/api';
import { formatPrice } from '@/lib/game';
import type { InventoryItem, LiveDrop, Skin } from '@/types';
import { Link, router, usePage } from '@inertiajs/vue3';
import { FileText, Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next';
import { computed, ref } from 'vue';

const props = defineProps<{
    liveDrops: LiveDrop[];
    inventory: InventoryItem[];
}>();

const page = usePage();
const isLoggedIn = computed(() => !!(page.props.auth as { user?: unknown })?.user);

const selectedIds = ref<number[]>([]);
const minSkins = 3;
const maxSkins = 10;
const isExecuting = ref(false);
const resultSkin = ref<Skin | null>(null);
const errorMessage = ref('');

const selectedItems = computed(() => props.inventory.filter((item) => selectedIds.value.includes(item.id)));

const totalValue = computed(() => selectedItems.value.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.skin.price, 0));

const contractResult = computed(() => {
    if (selectedItems.value.length < minSkins) return null;
    const avg = totalValue.value / selectedItems.value.length;
    return {
        min: Math.round(avg * 0.5),
        max: Math.round(avg * 2.5),
    };
});

function toggleItem(item: InventoryItem) {
    const idx = selectedIds.value.indexOf(item.id);
    if (idx >= 0) {
        selectedIds.value.splice(idx, 1);
    } else if (selectedIds.value.length < maxSkins) {
        selectedIds.value.push(item.id);
    }
}

function isSelected(item: InventoryItem) {
    return selectedIds.value.includes(item.id);
}

async function executeContract() {
    if (selectedIds.value.length < minSkins || isExecuting.value) return;

    if (!isLoggedIn.value) {
        router.visit('/login');
        return;
    }

    isExecuting.value = true;
    resultSkin.value = null;
    errorMessage.value = '';

    try {
        const data = await apiPost<{ skin: Skin }>('/contracts', {
            inventory_item_ids: selectedIds.value,
        });

        resultSkin.value = data.skin;
        selectedIds.value = [];
        router.reload({ only: ['inventory'] });
    } catch (error) {
        errorMessage.value = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'РћС€РёР±РєР° РєРѕРЅС‚СЂР°РєС‚Р°';
    } finally {
        isExecuting.value = false;
    }
}
</script>

<script>
import SkinItem from '../components/SkinItem.vue';
import { fireWinConfetti } from '../helpers/confetti.js';
import { calcContractRange, calcMaxContractBoost, formatPrice } from '../helpers/game.js';
import { sounds } from '../helpers/sounds.js';

export default {
    name: 'ContractsPage',
    components: { SkinItem },
    props: ['datasend', 'datasendJson', 'user', 'skinImg', 'imgError', 'rarityClass', 'rarityLabel', 'userInfo'],
    data() {
        return {
            inventory: [],
            selectedIds: [],
            minSkins: 2,
            maxSkins: 10,
            burning: false,
            wonSkin: null,
            message: null,
            boostAmount: 0,
        };
    },
    computed: {
        selectedItems() {
            return this.inventory.filter((item) => this.selectedIds.includes(item.id));
        },
        totalValue() {
            return this.selectedItems.reduce(function (sum, item) {
                return sum + item.skin.price;
            }, 0);
        },
        maxBoost() {
            if (!this.userInfo || this.userInfo.balance === undefined) {
                return 0;
            }
            return calcMaxContractBoost(this.userInfo.balance);
        },
        expectedRange() {
            if (this.selectedItems.length < this.minSkins) {
                return null;
            }
            return calcContractRange(this.totalValue, this.selectedItems.length, this.boostAmount);
        },
    },
    mounted() {
        if (this.user) {
            this.loadInventory();
        }
    },
    watch: {
        user(val) {
            if (val) {
                this.loadInventory();
            }
        },
    },
    methods: {
        formatPrice,
        loadInventory() {
            this.datasend('inventory').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.inventory = result;
                }
            });
        },
        toggle(id) {
            sounds.click();
            const index = this.selectedIds.indexOf(id);
            if (index >= 0) {
                this.selectedIds.splice(index, 1);
            } else if (this.selectedIds.length < this.maxSkins) {
                this.selectedIds.push(id);
            }
            this.clampBoost();
        },
        clearAll() {
            this.selectedIds = [];
        },
        clampBoost() {
            if (this.boostAmount > this.maxBoost) {
                this.boostAmount = this.maxBoost;
            }
        },
        execute() {
            sounds.contract();
            this.burning = true;
            this.wonSkin = null;
            this.message = null;
            this.datasendJson('contract', 'POST', {
                inventory_item_ids: this.selectedIds,
                boost_amount: this.boostAmount,
            }).then((result) => {
                setTimeout(() => {
                    this.burning = false;
                    if (!result) {
                        return;
                    }
                    if (result.message) {
                        this.message = result.message;
                        sounds.lose();
                        return;
                    }
                    this.wonSkin = result.skin;
                    this.selectedIds = [];
                    this.boostAmount = 0;
                    sounds.win();
                    fireWinConfetti();
                    this.loadInventory();
                }, 1200);
            });
        },
    },
};
</script>

<template>
    <div class="container-fluid px-lg-4 ludo-page">
        <div class="ludo-hero">
            <div class="row align-items-center">
                <div class="col-lg-8">
                    <h1>РћС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°Р№ РєРµР№СЃС‹ РЅР° <em>Р›СѓРґРѕРјР°РЅРёРё</em></h1>
                    <p>CS2 СЃРєРёРЅС‹, Р±РёС‚РІС‹, Р°РїРіСЂРµР№Рґ Рё РєРѕРЅС‚СЂР°РєС‚С‹ вЂ” РІСЃС‘ РІ РѕРґРЅРѕРј РјРµСЃС‚Рµ.</p>
                </div>
                <div class="col-lg-4 mt-3 mt-lg-0 text-lg-end">
                    <span class="ludo-stat-pill me-2">РљРµР№СЃРѕРІ: <strong>{{ cases.length }}</strong></span>
                    <span class="ludo-stat-pill">+30% Рє РїРµСЂРІРѕРјСѓ РґРµРїРѕР·РёС‚Сѓ</span>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="ludo-filter-tabs mb-4">
            <button
                v-for="tab in filterTabs"
                :key="tab.key"
                class="ludo-filter-tab"
                :class="{ active: category === tab.key }"
                @click="setCategory(tab.key)"
            >
                {{ tab.label }}
            </button>
        </div>

        <template v-for="section in visibleSections" :key="section.key">
            <div class="ludo-home-section" :id="'cases-' + section.key">
                <div class="ludo-section-label mb-3">{{ section.label }}</div>
                <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
                    <div v-for="item in section.cases" :key="item.id" class="col-6 col-md-4 col-lg-3 col-xl-2">
                        <div class="ludo-case-card" :class="{ 'ludo-card-featured': item.featured }" @click="openCase(item.id)">
                            <div class="ludo-case-img-wrap">
                                <img :src="caseImg(item.image)" :alt="item.name" @error="imgError($event, 'case')" />
                            </div>
                            <div class="ludo-case-card-body">
                                <div class="ludo-case-name">{{ item.name }}</div>
                                <div class="d-flex justify-content-center align-items-center gap-2 mt-1">
                                    <span class="ludo-case-price mb-0">{{ item.price }} в‚Ѕ</span>
                                    <span v-if="item.featured" class="ludo-case-badge">РҐРёС‚</span>
                                </div>
                                <button class="btn btn-ludo-primary btn-sm ludo-case-card-btn">РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </template>
    </div>
</template>

<script>
import { categoryLabels, categoryOrder } from '../helpers/game.js';

export default {
    name: 'HomePage',
    props: ['changePage', 'datasend', 'user', 'userInfo', 'getUser', 'caseImg', 'imgError'],
    data() {
        return {
            cases: [],
            category: 'all',
        };
    },
    mounted() {
        this.getCases();
    },
    methods: {
        getCases() {
            this.datasend('cases').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.cases = result;
                }
            });
        },
        setCategory(key) {
            this.category = key;
            if (key !== 'all') {
                this.$nextTick(() => {
                    document.getElementById('cases-' + key)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
                });
            }
        },
        openCase(id) {
            this.changePage('CasePage', id);
        },
        sortByPriceDesc(list) {
            return [...list].sort((left, right) => right.price - left.price || left.name.localeCompare(right.name));
        },
    },
    computed: {
        filterTabs() {
            return [
                { key: 'all', label: categoryLabels.all },
                ...categoryOrder.map((key) => ({ key, label: categoryLabels[key] })),
            ];
        },
        categorySections() {
            return categoryOrder
                .map((key) => ({
                    key,
                    label: categoryLabels[key],
                    cases: this.sortByPriceDesc(this.cases.filter((item) => item.category === key)),
                }))
                .filter((section) => section.cases.length > 0);
        },
        visibleSections() {
            if (this.category === 'all') {
                return this.categorySections;
            }

            return this.categorySections.filter((section) => section.key === this.category);
        },
    },
};
</script>

<script>
import UserAvatar from '../components/UserAvatar.vue';

export default {
    name: 'PlayerPage',
    components: { UserAvatar },
    props: ['changePage', 'datasend', 'pageId', 'skinImg', 'imgError'],
    data() {
        return {
            profile: null,
        };
    },
    mounted() {
        this.loadProfile();
    },
    watch: {
        pageId() {
            this.loadProfile();
        },
    },
    methods: {
        loadProfile() {
            if (!this.pageId) {
                return;
            }
            this.profile = null;
            this.datasend('users/' + this.pageId).then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.profile = result;
                }
            });
        },
        formatDate(iso) {
            if (!iso) {
                return 'вЂ”';
            }
            return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('ru-RU', {
                day: '2-digit',
                month: '2-digit',
                year: 'numeric',
            });
        },
    },
};
</script>

                <div class="ludo-section-label text-success mb-3">РўРµР±Рµ РІС‹РїР°Р»Рѕ!</div>
                <img :src="skinImg(wonSkin.image)" class="ludo-secret-win-img mb-2" @error="imgError($event, 'skin')" />
                <div class="fw-semibold">{{ wonSkin.weapon }} | {{ wonSkin.name }}</div>
                <div class="ludo-case-price">{{ wonSkin.price }} в‚Ѕ</div>
            </div>

            <div v-if="message" class="ludo-alert ludo-alert-info mb-3 text-center">{{ message }}</div>

            <div class="text-center">
                <button
                    v-if="user"
                    class="btn btn-ludo-primary btn-lg px-5"
                    :disabled="loading || secondsLeft > 0"
                    @click="openSecret"
                >
                    <template v-if="loading">РљСЂСѓС‚РёРј...</template>
                    <template v-else-if="secondsLeft > 0">РџРѕРґРѕР¶РґРёС‚Рµ {{ formatCooldown(secondsLeft) }}</template>
                    <template v-else>РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ Р±РµСЃРїР»Р°С‚РЅРѕ</template>
                </button>
                <button v-else class="btn btn-ludo-primary btn-lg" @click="changePage('AuthPage')">Р’РѕР№С‚Рё, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ</button>
            </div>
        </template>
    </div>

<script>
import { sounds } from '../helpers/sounds.js';

export default {
    name: 'SecretCasePage',
    props: ['changePage', 'datasend', 'user', 'getUser', 'onLiveDrop', 'skinImg', 'caseImg', 'imgError', 'rarityClass'],
    data() {
        return {
            data: null,
            loading: false,
            message: null,
            wonSkin: null,
            secondsLeft: 0,
            cooldownInterval: null,
        };
    },
    mounted() {
        this.loadCase();
        this.cooldownInterval = setInterval(() => this.tickCooldown(), 1000);
    },
    unmounted() {
        clearInterval(this.cooldownInterval);
    },
    methods: {
        loadCase() {
            this.datasend('secret-case').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.data = result;
                    this.syncCooldown();
                }
            });
        },
        syncCooldown() {
            if (!this.data || !this.data.readyAt) {
                this.secondsLeft = this.data ? (this.data.secondsLeft || 0) : 0;
                return;
            }
            this.secondsLeft = Math.max(0, this.data.readyAt - Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
        },
        tickCooldown() {
            if (this.secondsLeft > 0) {
                this.secondsLeft -= 1;
            }
        },
        formatCooldown(seconds) {
            const m = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
            const s = seconds % 60;
            if (m > 0) {
                return m + 'Рј ' + s + 'СЃ';
            }
            return s + 'СЃ';
        },
        openSecret() {
            if (this.loading || this.secondsLeft > 0) {
                return;
            }
            this.loading = true;
            this.message = null;
            this.wonSkin = null;
            this.datasend('secret-case/open', 'POST', new FormData()).then((result) => {
                this.loading = false;
                if (!result) {
                    return;
                }
                if (result.message) {
                    this.message = result.message;
                    return;
                }
                sounds.win();
                this.wonSkin = result.skin;
                if (result.liveDrop && this.onLiveDrop) {
                    this.onLiveDrop(result.liveDrop);
                }
                if (result.readyAt) {
                    this.data.readyAt = result.readyAt;
                    this.syncCooldown();
                } else {
                    this.secondsLeft = result.cooldownSeconds || 1800;
                }
                this.getUser();
            });
        },
    },
};
</script>

<script setup lang="ts">
import SkinItem from '@/components/SkinItem.vue';
import CaseBattleLayout from '@/layouts/CaseBattleLayout.vue';
import { apiPost } from '@/lib/api';
import { formatPrice } from '@/lib/game';
import type { InventoryItem, LiveDrop, Skin } from '@/types';
import { Link, router, usePage } from '@inertiajs/vue3';
import { ArrowRight, TrendingUp } from 'lucide-vue-next';
import { computed, ref } from 'vue';

const props = defineProps<{
    liveDrops: LiveDrop[];
    inventory: InventoryItem[];
}>();

const page = usePage();
const isLoggedIn = computed(() => !!(page.props.auth as { user?: unknown })?.user);

const selectedItem = ref<InventoryItem | null>(null);
const targetPrice = ref(5000);
const upgradeChance = ref(15);
const isUpgrading = ref(false);
const upgradeResult = ref<'win' | 'lose' | null>(null);
const wonSkin = ref<Skin | null>(null);
const errorMessage = ref('');

const selectedSkin = computed(() => selectedItem.value?.skin ?? null);

function selectItem(item: InventoryItem) {
    selectedItem.value = item;
    targetPrice.value = Math.round(item.skin.price * 3);
    updateChance();
}

function updateChance() {
    if (!selectedSkin.value) return;
    upgradeChance.value = Math.min(75, Math.max(5, Math.round((selectedSkin.value.price / targetPrice.value) * 100)));
}

async function startUpgrade() {
    if (!selectedItem.value || isUpgrading.value) return;

    if (!isLoggedIn.value) {
        router.visit('/login');
        return;
    }

    isUpgrading.value = true;
    upgradeResult.value = null;
    wonSkin.value = null;
    errorMessage.value = '';

    try {
        const data = await apiPost<{ success: boolean; skin?: Skin }>('/upgrade', {
            inventory_item_id: selectedItem.value.id,
            target_price: targetPrice.value,
        });

        upgradeResult.value = data.success ? 'win' : 'lose';
        if (data.success && data.skin) {
            wonSkin.value = data.skin;
        }
        selectedItem.value = null;
        router.reload({ only: ['inventory'] });
    } catch (error) {
        errorMessage.value = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'РћС€РёР±РєР° Р°РїРіСЂРµР№РґР°';
    } finally {
        isUpgrading.value = false;
    }
}
</script>

<script>
import SkinItem from '../components/SkinItem.vue';
import { fireUpgradeConfetti } from '../helpers/confetti.js';
import {
    calcUpgradeChance,
    calcMaxUpgradeBoost,
    calcUpgradeBaseChance,
    calcUpgradeBoostStep,
    formatPrice,
    isUpgradeTargetAllowed,
    UPGRADE_MAX_CHANCE,
    UPGRADE_MIN_CHANCE,
} from '../helpers/game.js';
import { rarityOrder } from '../helpers/images-meta.js';
import { calcUpgradeArrowAngle } from '../helpers/upgradeSpin.js';
import { sounds } from '../helpers/sounds.js';

const SPIN_MS = 4500;
const FAST_SPIN_MS = 600;

export default {
    name: 'UpgradePage',
    components: { SkinItem },
    props: ['datasend', 'datasendJson', 'user', 'userInfo', 'getUser', 'changePage', 'skinImg', 'imgError', 'rarityClass', 'rarityLabel'],
    data() {
        return {
            inventory: [],
            catalog: [],
            selectedId: null,
            selectedItem: null,
            targetSkinId: null,
            targetSkin: null,
            targetFilter: 'all',
            boostAmount: 0,
            chance: 0,
            fastUpgrade: false,
            isUpgrading: false,
            result: null,
            wonSkin: null,
            rarityFilters: rarityOrder,
            spinTimer: null,
            arrowAngle: 0,
            pendingUpgradeResult: null,
        };
    },
    computed: {
        balance() {
            return Number(this.userInfo?.balance) || 0;
        },
        baseChance() {
            if (!this.selectedItem || !this.targetSkin) {
                return 0;
            }
            return calcUpgradeBaseChance(this.selectedItem.skin.price, this.targetSkin.price);
        },
        maxChance() {
            return UPGRADE_MAX_CHANCE;
        },
        minChance() {
            return UPGRADE_MIN_CHANCE;
        },
        chanceBoostRoom() {
            return Math.max(0, UPGRADE_MAX_CHANCE - this.baseChance);
        },
        boostStep() {
            if (!this.selectedItem || !this.targetSkin) {
                return 50;
            }
            return calcUpgradeBoostStep(this.selectedItem.skin.price, this.targetSkin.price);
        },
        fullBoostCost() {
            if (!this.selectedItem || !this.targetSkin) {
                return 0;
            }
            return calcMaxUpgradeBoost(
                this.selectedItem.skin.price,
                this.targetSkin.price,
                Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER,
            );
        },
        maxBoostByChance() {
            return this.fullBoostCost;
        },
        maxBoost() {
            if (!this.userInfo || this.userInfo.balance === undefined) {
                return 0;
            }
            if (!this.selectedItem || !this.targetSkin) {
                return calcMaxUpgradeBoost(1, 2, this.balance);
            }
            return calcMaxUpgradeBoost(
                this.selectedItem.skin.price,
                this.targetSkin.price,
                this.balance,
            );
        },
        boostLimitedByBalance() {
            return this.chanceBoostRoom > 0 && this.maxBoost > 0 && this.maxBoost < this.maxBoostByChance;
        },
        boostChanceBonus() {
            return Math.max(0, this.chance - this.baseChance);
        },
        winZoneDash() {
            const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * 42;
            const winLength = (this.chance / 100) * circumference;
            return `${winLength} ${circumference}`;
        },
        filteredTargets() {
            if (!this.selectedItem) {
                return [];
            }
            const minPrice = this.selectedItem.skin.price;
            let list = this.catalog.filter((skin) => {
                return skin.price > minPrice && isUpgradeTargetAllowed(minPrice, skin.price);
            });
            if (this.targetFilter !== 'all') {
                list = list.filter((skin) => skin.rarity === this.targetFilter);
            }
            return list;
        },
    },
    mounted() {
        if (this.user) {
            this.loadData();
        }
    },
    unmounted() {
        clearTimeout(this.spinTimer);
    },
    watch: {
        user(val) {
            if (val) {
                this.loadData();
            }
        },
        maxBoost() {
            this.clampBoost();
            this.updateChance();
        },
        'userInfo.balance'() {
            this.clampBoost();
            this.updateChance();
        },
    },
    methods: {
        formatPrice,
        loadData() {
            this.datasend('inventory').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.inventory = result;
                }
            });
            this.datasend('skins').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.catalog = result;
                }
            });
        },
        setTargetFilter(rarity) {
            sounds.click();
            this.targetFilter = rarity;
        },
        selectSource(item) {
            sounds.click();
            this.selectedId = item.id;
            this.selectedItem = item;
            this.result = null;
            this.wonSkin = null;

            if (this.targetSkin && this.targetSkin.price <= item.skin.price) {
                this.targetSkinId = null;
                this.targetSkin = null;
                this.chance = 0;
                this.boostAmount = 0;
            } else if (this.targetSkin && !isUpgradeTargetAllowed(item.skin.price, this.targetSkin.price)) {
                this.targetSkinId = null;
                this.targetSkin = null;
                this.chance = 0;
                this.boostAmount = 0;
            } else {
                this.clampBoost();
                this.updateChance();
            }
        },
        selectTarget(skin) {
            if (!this.selectedItem || skin.price <= this.selectedItem.skin.price) {
                return;
            }
            if (!isUpgradeTargetAllowed(this.selectedItem.skin.price, skin.price)) {
                return;
            }
            sounds.click();
            this.targetSkinId = skin.id;
            this.targetSkin = skin;
            this.result = null;
            this.wonSkin = null;
            this.clampBoost();
            this.updateChance();
        },
        clampBoost() {
            const cap = Math.max(0, Number(this.maxBoost) || 0);
            if (this.boostAmount > cap) {
                this.boostAmount = cap;
            }
            if (this.boostAmount < 0 || Number.isNaN(this.boostAmount)) {
                this.boostAmount = 0;
            }
        },
        onBoostInput() {
            this.clampBoost();
            this.updateChance();
        },
        updateChance() {
            if (!this.selectedItem || !this.targetSkin) {
                this.chance = 0;
                return;
            }
            this.chance = calcUpgradeChance(this.selectedItem.skin.price, this.targetSkin.price, this.boostAmount);
        },
        finishUpgrade(result) {
            this.isUpgrading = false;
            if (!result) {
                return;
            }
            if (result.message) {
                alert(result.message);
                return;
            }
            this.result = result.success;
            if (result.success) {
                this.wonSkin = result.skin;
                sounds.win();
                fireUpgradeConfetti(true);
            } else {
                sounds.lose();
            }
            this.selectedId = null;
            this.selectedItem = null;
            this.targetSkinId = null;
            this.targetSkin = null;
            this.boostAmount = 0;
            this.chance = 0;
            if (this.getUser) {
                this.getUser();
            }
            this.loadData();
        },
        upgrade() {
            if (!this.selectedId || !this.targetSkinId || this.isUpgrading) {
                return;
            }
            sounds.upgrade();
            this.isUpgrading = true;
            this.result = null;
            this.wonSkin = null;
            this.pendingUpgradeResult = null;

            this.datasendJson('upgrade', 'POST', {
                inventory_item_id: this.selectedId,
                target_skin_id: this.targetSkinId,
                boost_amount: this.boostAmount,
            }).then((result) => {
                if (!result) {
                    this.isUpgrading = false;
                    return;
                }
                if (result.message) {
                    this.isUpgrading = false;
                    alert(result.message);
                    return;
                }

                this.pendingUpgradeResult = result;
                const startAngle = this.arrowAngle % 360;
                const targetAngle = calcUpgradeArrowAngle(this.chance, !!result.success);
                this.arrowAngle = this.arrowAngle - startAngle + targetAngle;

                clearTimeout(this.spinTimer);
                this.spinTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                    this.finishUpgrade(this.pendingUpgradeResult);
                    this.pendingUpgradeResult = null;
                }, this.fastUpgrade ? FAST_SPIN_MS : SPIN_MS);
            });
        },
    },
};
</script>
