<script>
export default {
    name: 'UserPage',
    props: ['changePage', 'datasend', 'datasendJson', 'user', 'getUser', 'openDeposit', 'skinImg', 'imgError', 'sourceLabel', 'txType'],
    data() {
        return {
            profile: null,
            withdrawIds: [],
            promoSeconds: 0,
            promoInterval: null,
            showTransactions: false,
            uploadingAvatar: false,
            sellingAll: false,
        };
    },
    computed: {
        initials() {
            if (!this.profile) return '??';
            const parts = this.profile.user.name.trim().split(/[_\s]+/);
            if (parts.length >= 2) return (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();
            return this.profile.user.name.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
        },
        withdrawSelection() {
            if (!this.profile) return [];
            return this.profile.inventory.filter((item) => this.withdrawIds.includes(item.id));
        },
        withdrawTotal() {
            return this.withdrawSelection.reduce(function (sum, item) {
                return sum + item.skin.price;
            }, 0);
        },
    },
    mounted() {
        if (this.user) {
            this.loadProfile();
        }
        this.promoInterval = setInterval(() => this.syncPromoTimer(), 1000);
    },
    unmounted() {
        clearInterval(this.promoInterval);
    },
    watch: {
        user(val) {
            if (val) {
                this.loadProfile();
            }
        },
    },
    methods: {
        loadProfile() {
            this.datasend('user/profile').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.profile = result;
                    this.syncPromoTimer();
                }
            });
        },
        syncPromoTimer() {
            if (!this.profile || !this.profile.promos || !this.profile.promos.rotating) {
                return;
            }
            this.promoSeconds = Math.max(0, this.profile.promos.rotating.expiresAt - Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
        },
        formatDate(iso) {
            if (!iso) return 'вЂ”';
            return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('ru-RU', {
                day: '2-digit',
                month: '2-digit',
                year: 'numeric',
            });
        },
        uploadAvatar(event) {
            const file = event.target.files && event.target.files[0];
            if (!file) {
                return;
            }
            this.uploadingAvatar = true;
            let fd = new FormData();
            fd.append('avatar', file);
            this.datasend('user/avatar', 'POST', fd).then((result) => {
                this.uploadingAvatar = false;
                if (result && result.avatar !== undefined) {
                    this.profile.user.avatar = result.avatar;
                    this.getUser();
                }
            });
        },
        removeAvatar() {
            this.datasend('user/avatar', 'DELETE').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.profile.user.avatar = null;
                    this.getUser();
                }
            });
        },
        toggleWithdraw(id) {
            const index = this.withdrawIds.indexOf(id);
            if (index >= 0) {
                this.withdrawIds.splice(index, 1);
            } else {
                this.withdrawIds.push(id);
            }
        },
        sellItem(id) {
            if (!confirm('РџСЂРѕРґР°С‚СЊ СЃРєРёРЅ Р·Р° РµРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ?')) return;
            this.datasend('inventory/' + id + '/sell', 'POST', new FormData()).then(() => {
                this.getUser();
                this.loadProfile();
            });
        },
        sellAllItems() {
            if (!this.profile || !this.profile.inventory.length) return;
            const total = this.profile.stats.inventoryValue;
            const count = this.profile.inventory.length;
            if (!confirm('РџСЂРѕРґР°С‚СЊ РІСЃРµ ' + count + ' СЃРєРёРЅ(РѕРІ) Р·Р° ' + total + ' в‚Ѕ?')) return;
            this.sellingAll = true;
            this.datasend('inventory/sell-all', 'POST', new FormData()).then((result) => {
                this.sellingAll = false;
                if (!result) return;
                this.withdrawIds = [];
                this.getUser();
                this.loadProfile();
            });
        },
        withdrawSkins() {
            if (!this.withdrawIds.length) return;
            if (!confirm('РЎРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ Р·Р°СЏРІРєСѓ РЅР° РІС‹РІРѕРґ ' + this.withdrawIds.length + ' СЃРєРёРЅ(РѕРІ) РЅР° ' + this.withdrawTotal + ' в‚Ѕ? РЎРєРёРЅС‹ СѓР№РґСѓС‚ РЅР° РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєСѓ Р°РґРјРёРЅСѓ.')) {
                return;
            }
            this.datasendJson('inventory/withdraw', 'POST', {
                inventory_item_ids: this.withdrawIds,
            }).then((result) => {
                if (!result) return;
                alert(result.message || 'Р—Р°СЏРІРєР° РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅР°');
                this.withdrawIds = [];
                this.getUser();
                this.loadProfile();
            });
        },
    },
};
</script>

} satisfies RouteDefinition<["get","head"]>

/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\Admin\CaseController::edit
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CaseController.php:44
 * @route '/admin/cases/{gameCase}/edit'
 */
edit.url = (args: { gameCase: number | { id: number } } | [gameCase: number | { id: number } ] | number | { id: number }, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { gameCase: args }
    }

            if (typeof args === 'object' && !Array.isArray(args) && 'id' in args) {
            args = { gameCase: args.id }
        }
    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    gameCase: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        gameCase: typeof args.gameCase === 'object'
                ? args.gameCase.id
                : args.gameCase,
                }

    return edit.definition.url
            .replace('{gameCase}', parsedArgs.gameCase.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

    method: 'get',

    methods: ["get","head"],
    url: '/admin/skins/{skin}/edit',

/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\Admin\SkinController::edit
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SkinController.php:38
 * @route '/admin/skins/{skin}/edit'
 */
edit.url = (args: { skin: number | { id: number } } | [skin: number | { id: number } ] | number | { id: number }, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { skin: args }
    }

            if (typeof args === 'object' && !Array.isArray(args) && 'id' in args) {
            args = { skin: args.id }
        }
    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    skin: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        skin: typeof args.skin === 'object'
                ? args.skin.id
                : args.skin,
                }

    return edit.definition.url
            .replace('{skin}', parsedArgs.skin.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

import { queryParams, type RouteQueryOptions, type RouteDefinition, type RouteFormDefinition, applyUrlDefaults } from './../../wayfinder'
/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\GameController::show
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/GameController.php:49
 * @route '/case/{id}'
 */
export const show = (args: { id: string | number } | [id: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteDefinition<'get'> => ({
    url: show.url(args, options),
    method: 'get',
})

show.definition = {
    methods: ["get","head"],
    url: '/case/{id}',
} satisfies RouteDefinition<["get","head"]>

/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\GameController::show
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/GameController.php:49
 * @route '/case/{id}'
 */
show.url = (args: { id: string | number } | [id: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { id: args }
    }

    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    id: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        id: args.id,
                }

    return show.definition.url
            .replace('{id}', parsedArgs.id.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

edit.url = (args: { gameCase: number | { id: number } } | [gameCase: number | { id: number } ] | number | { id: number }, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { gameCase: args }
    }

            if (typeof args === 'object' && !Array.isArray(args) && 'id' in args) {
            args = { gameCase: args.id }
        }

                        gameCase: typeof args.gameCase === 'object'
                ? args.gameCase.id
                : args.gameCase,

            .replace('{gameCase}', parsedArgs.gameCase.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)

/**
* @see \App\Http\Controllers\Manager\CaseController::featured
 * @see app/Http/Controllers/Manager/CaseController.php:58
 * @route '/manager/cases/{gameCase}/featured'
 */
featured.url = (args: { gameCase: number | { id: number } } | [gameCase: number | { id: number } ] | number | { id: number }, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { gameCase: args }
    }

            if (typeof args === 'object' && !Array.isArray(args) && 'id' in args) {
            args = { gameCase: args.id }
        }
    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    gameCase: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        gameCase: typeof args.gameCase === 'object'
                ? args.gameCase.id
                : args.gameCase,
                }

    return featured.definition.url
            .replace('{gameCase}', parsedArgs.gameCase.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

 * @see vendor/laravel/fortify/src/Http/Controllers/NewPasswordController.php:44
 * @route '/reset-password/{token}'
 */
reset.url = (args: { token: string | number } | [token: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { token: args }
    }

    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    token: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        token: args.token,
                }

    return reset.definition.url
            .replace('{token}', parsedArgs.token.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

import { queryParams, type RouteQueryOptions, type RouteDefinition, type RouteFormDefinition, applyUrlDefaults } from './../../../wayfinder'
/**
 * @see vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Filesystem/FilesystemServiceProvider.php:106
 * @route '/storage/{path}'
 */
export const upload = (args: { path: string | number } | [path: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteDefinition<'put'> => ({
    url: upload.url(args, options),
    method: 'put',
})

upload.definition = {
    methods: ["put"],
    url: '/storage/{path}',
} satisfies RouteDefinition<["put"]>

/**
 * @see vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Filesystem/FilesystemServiceProvider.php:106
 * @route '/storage/{path}'
 */
upload.url = (args: { path: string | number } | [path: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (typeof args === 'string' || typeof args === 'number') {
        args = { path: args }
    }

    
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    path: args[0],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        path: args.path,
                }

    return upload.definition.url
            .replace('{path}', parsedArgs.path.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

/**
 * @see vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Filesystem/FilesystemServiceProvider.php:106
 * @route '/storage/{path}'
 */
upload.put = (args: { path: string | number } | [path: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteDefinition<'put'> => ({
    url: upload.url(args, options),
    method: 'put',
})

    /**
 * @see vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Filesystem/FilesystemServiceProvider.php:106
 * @route '/storage/{path}'
 */
    const uploadForm = (args: { path: string | number } | [path: string | number ] | string | number, options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteFormDefinition<'post'> => ({
        action: upload.url(args, {
                    [options?.mergeQuery ? 'mergeQuery' : 'query']: {
                        _method: 'PUT',
                        ...(options?.query ?? options?.mergeQuery ?? {}),
                    }
                }),
        method: 'post',
    })

            /**
 * @see vendor/laravel/framework/src/Illuminate/Filesystem/FilesystemServiceProvider.php:106

* @see \Laravel\Fortify\Http\Controllers\VerifyEmailController::verify
 * @see vendor/laravel/fortify/src/Http/Controllers/VerifyEmailController.php:18
 * @route '/email/verify/{id}/{hash}'
 */
export const verify = (args: { id: string | number, hash: string | number } | [id: string | number, hash: string | number ], options?: RouteQueryOptions): RouteDefinition<'get'> => ({
    url: verify.url(args, options),
    method: 'get',
})

verify.definition = {
    methods: ["get","head"],
    url: '/email/verify/{id}/{hash}',
} satisfies RouteDefinition<["get","head"]>

/**
* @see \Laravel\Fortify\Http\Controllers\VerifyEmailController::verify
 * @see vendor/laravel/fortify/src/Http/Controllers/VerifyEmailController.php:18
 * @route '/email/verify/{id}/{hash}'
 */
verify.url = (args: { id: string | number, hash: string | number } | [id: string | number, hash: string | number ], options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (Array.isArray(args)) {
        args = {
                    id: args[0],
                    hash: args[1],
                }
    }

    args = applyUrlDefaults(args)

    const parsedArgs = {
                        id: args.id,
                                hash: args.hash,
                }

    return verify.definition.url
            .replace('{id}', parsedArgs.id.toString())
            .replace('{hash}', parsedArgs.hash.toString())
            .replace(/\/+$/, '') + queryParams(options)
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\Виталя\Desktop\лудомания — копия\resources\js\wayfinder\index.ts ===
export type QueryParams = {
    [key: string]:
        | string
        | number
        | boolean
        | (string | number)[]
        | null
        | undefined
        | QueryParams;
};

type Method = "get" | "post" | "put" | "delete" | "patch" | "head" | "options";
type UrlDefaults = Record<string, unknown>;

let urlDefaults: () => UrlDefaults = () => ({});

export type RouteDefinition<TMethod extends Method | Method[]> = {
    url: string;
} & (TMethod extends Method[] ? { methods: TMethod } : { method: TMethod });

export type RouteFormDefinition<TMethod extends Method> = {
    action: string;
    method: TMethod;
};

export type RouteQueryOptions = {
    query?: QueryParams;
    mergeQuery?: QueryParams;
};

const getValue = (value: string | number | boolean) => {
    if (value === true) {
        return "1";
    }

    if (value === false) {
        return "0";
    }

    return value.toString();
};

const addNestedParams = (
    obj: QueryParams,
    prefix: string,
    params: URLSearchParams,
) => {
    Object.entries(obj).forEach(([subKey, value]) => {
        if (value === undefined) return;

        const paramKey = `${prefix}[${subKey}]`;

        if (Array.isArray(value)) {
            value.forEach((v) => params.append(`${paramKey}[]`, getValue(v)));
        } else if (value !== null && typeof value === "object") {
            addNestedParams(value, paramKey, params);
        } else if (["string", "number", "boolean"].includes(typeof value)) {
            params.set(paramKey, getValue(value as string | number | boolean));
        }
    });
};

const clearParamFamily = (params: URLSearchParams, key: string) => {
    const toDelete = new Set<string>();

    params.forEach((_, paramKey) => {
        if (paramKey === key || paramKey.startsWith(`${key}[`)) {
            toDelete.add(paramKey);
        }
    });

    toDelete.forEach((paramKey) => params.delete(paramKey));
};

export const queryParams = (options?: RouteQueryOptions) => {
    if (!options || (!options.query && !options.mergeQuery)) {
        return "";
    }

    const query = options.query ?? options.mergeQuery;
    const includeExisting = options.mergeQuery !== undefined;

    const params = new URLSearchParams(
        includeExisting && typeof window !== "undefined"
            ? window.location.search
            : "",
    );

    for (const key in query) {
        const queryValue = query[key];

        if (includeExisting) {
            clearParamFamily(params, key);
        }

        if (queryValue === undefined || queryValue === null) {
            continue;
        }

        if (Array.isArray(queryValue)) {
            queryValue.forEach((value) => {
                params.append(`${key}[]`, value.toString());
            });
        } else if (typeof queryValue === "object") {
            addNestedParams(queryValue, key, params);
        } else {
            params.set(key, getValue(queryValue));
        }
    }

    const str = params.toString();

    return str.length > 0 ? `?${str}` : "";
};

export const setUrlDefaults = (params: UrlDefaults | (() => UrlDefaults)) => {
    urlDefaults = typeof params === "function" ? params : () => params;
};

export const addUrlDefault = (
    key: string,
    value: string | number | boolean,
) => {
    const previousDefaults = urlDefaults;

    urlDefaults = () => ({
        ...previousDefaults(),
        [key]: value,
    });
};

export const applyUrlDefaults = <T extends UrlDefaults | undefined>(
    existing: T,
): T => {
    const existingParams = { ...(existing ?? ({} as UrlDefaults)) };
    const defaultParams = urlDefaults();

    for (const key in defaultParams) {
        if (
            existingParams[key] === undefined &&
            defaultParams[key] !== undefined
        ) {
            (existingParams as Record<string, unknown>)[key] =
                defaultParams[key];
        }
    }

    return existingParams as T;
};

export const validateParameters = (
    args: Record<string, unknown> | undefined,
    optional: string[],
) => {
    const missing = optional.filter((key) => {
        const value = args?.[key];

        return (
            value === undefined ||
            value === null ||
            value === "" ||
            value === false
        );
    });
    const expectedMissing = optional.slice(missing.length * -1);

    for (let i = 0; i < missing.length; i++) {
        if (missing[i] !== expectedMissing[i]) {
            throw Error(
                "Unexpected optional parameters missing. Unable to generate a URL.",
            );
        }
    }
};

                manageWithdrawals: false,
                manageFeatured: false,

        this.onlineTimer = setInterval(() => {

        this.setupEcho();
    },
    unmounted() {

        clearInterval(this.onlineTimer);
        clearInterval(this.promoTimer);

    methods: {

            });
        },
        setupEcho() {
            if (!window.Echo) {
                return;
            }
            window.Echo.channel('live-drops').listen('.LiveDropCreated', (e) => {
                if (e && e.drop) {
                    this.prependLiveDrop(e.drop);
                }
            });
        },
        prependLiveDrop(drop) {
            this.liveDrops = [drop, ...this.liveDrops.filter((d) => d.id !== drop.id)].slice(0, 30);
        },
        getLiveDrops() {
            this.datasend('live-drops').then((result) => {
                if (result) {
                    this.liveDrops = result;
                }
            });
        },
        getUser() {
            this.datasend('user').then((response) => {
                if (!response) {
                    return;
                }
                this.user = true;
                this.userInfo = response;
                this.permissions = {
                    manageRoles: false,
                    deleteUsers: false,
                    viewUsers: false,
                    manageSkins: false,
                    manageCases: false,
                    manageWithdrawals: false,
                    manageFeatured: false,
                    viewLiveDrops: false,
                    viewStats: false,
                    ...(response.permissions || {}),
                };
            });
        },
        changePage(page, pageId = null) {
            this.page = page;
            this.pageId = pageId;
            localStorage.setItem('page', page);
            localStorage.setItem('pageId', pageId);
        },
        logoutUser() {
            if (localStorage.getItem('token')) {
                this.datasend('logout', 'POST');
            }
            localStorage.removeItem('token');
            this.user = false;
            this.permissions = {
                manageRoles: false,
                deleteUsers: false,
                viewUsers: false,
                manageSkins: false,
                manageCases: false,
                manageWithdrawals: false,
                manageFeatured: false,
                viewLiveDrops: false,
                viewStats: false,
            };
            this.userInfo = {};
            this.changePage('HomePage');
        },
        changeToken(token) {
            localStorage.setItem('token', token);
            this.user = true;
            this.getUser();
        },
        async datasend(route, method = 'GET', formdata = null) {
            const myHeaders = new Headers();
            myHeaders.append('Accept', 'application/json');
            if (localStorage.getItem('token')) {
                myHeaders.append('Authorization', 'Bearer ' + localStorage.getItem('token'));
            }
            const requestOptions = {
                method: method,
                headers: myHeaders,
                redirect: 'follow',
            };
            if (method != 'GET') {
                requestOptions.body = formdata;
            }
            const response = await fetch(this.API + route, requestOptions);
            if (response.status == 401) {
                this.logoutUser();
                return;
            }
            if (response.status == 204) {
                return {};
            }
            return response.json();
        },
        async datasendJson(route, method = 'POST', data = null) {
            const myHeaders = new Headers();
            myHeaders.append('Accept', 'application/json');
            myHeaders.append('Content-Type', 'application/json');
            if (localStorage.getItem('token')) {
                myHeaders.append('Authorization', 'Bearer ' + localStorage.getItem('token'));
            }
            const response = await fetch(this.API + route, {
                method: method,
                headers: myHeaders,
                body: JSON.stringify(data),
            });
            if (response.status == 401) {
                this.logoutUser();
                return;
            }
            return response.json();
        },
    },
};
</script>

import Echo from 'laravel-echo';
import Pusher from 'pusher-js';

declare global {
    interface Window {
        Pusher: typeof Pusher;
        Echo?: Echo<'reverb'>;
    }
}

const key = import.meta.env.VITE_REVERB_APP_KEY as string | undefined;

let echo: Echo<'reverb'> | null = null;

if (key) {
    window.Pusher = Pusher;

    const scheme = import.meta.env.VITE_REVERB_SCHEME ?? 'http';
    const port = import.meta.env.VITE_REVERB_PORT ?? '8080';

    echo = new Echo({
        broadcaster: 'reverb',
        key,
        wsHost: import.meta.env.VITE_REVERB_HOST ?? 'localhost',
        wsPort: port,
        wssPort: port,
        forceTLS: scheme === 'https',
        enabledTransports: ['ws', 'wss'],
    });

    window.Echo = echo;
}

export { echo };
export default echo;
